   113	checked at every sampled step to C+H (observer neutrality, per the source's instrumented/plain requirement). Outcome =
   114	sign of (treated - control) of the readout at C+H against `minEffect`: up / down / none.
   115	
   116	Mechanism fixtures (outcome labels are *measured*, not assumed; any label that disagrees with its mechanism expectation
   117	is reported, not silently relabelled): feed (expect up), lesion on occupied region (expect down), drain (expect down),
   118	sham (none by construction), lesion on empty region (expect none; may differ due to fragmentation or edge effects, so the
   119	dev qualification run decides), and a low-dose feed (expect none by threshold). The earlier "saturated feed is none" idea
   120	is **dropped**: feed has no saturation clamp (`feed.ts` ~77-89).
   121	
   122	### 7. Splits and the freeze order (finding 7)
   123	Whole source families by `familyId` hashed with a frozen salt into `dev` / `test` (total histories 12, raised to 24 only if
   124	a dev timing measurement says so, decided before freeze). A separate reserved `qualify` subset of **dev** histories is used
   125	for all fixture qualification, `minEffect` choice, seed-flip stability, runtime measurement and proposer rule tuning.
   126	Freeze (protocol.json hashed into the manifest, written and committed before any test-split outcome is generated):
   127	family list and split membership, salt, seed reservations, `minEffect`, readout definitions, proposer rules/probs, donor
   128	assignments, denominators. Test outcomes are generated once by `predict run --stage confirm`, which refuses to run unless
   129	the protocol hash matches the frozen file. No test case is dropped for unstable outcome: unstable test cases stay in the
   130	unfiltered denominator, flagged `measurementLimited` (the prespecified category), and reported both with and without. Any
   131	post-inspection amendment needs fresh confirmation families and a dated amendment. Proposer code only receives envelopes;
   132	only the scorer reads outcomes. `assertNoLeak` checks disjoint family sets, that no test envelope contains dev data, and
   133	that shuffled donors obey the derangement rule.
   134	
   135	### 8. Scoring (`scoreRun`)
   136	- **Multiclass Brier** over `{up, down, none}`: `BS = (1/N) * sum_i sum_c (p_ic/1000 - o_ic)^2`, range 0..2. Abstentions
   137	  and invalid/missing predictions are imputed with the frozen **dev-split prior** distribution for the headline number
   138	  (so abstaining cannot beat the prior), with answered-only Brier also reported.
   139	- Directional accuracy on answered cases (argmax vs outcome), balanced accuracy over outcome classes, **accuracy at matched
   140	  coverage** (each comparator restricted to the cases the least-covering one answered, when that is non-empty),
   141	  coverage, abstention rate, invalid and missing counts, mean absolute effect (own units, reported per unit).
   142	- A **calibrated majority baseline is legitimately strong**: the table does not claim Brier punishes it; "strongest simple
   143	  baseline" is whichever of {priorMajority, alwaysNone, fixedRule, numericTrend, v0Rules} scores best on dev.
   144	- Reliability table over the stated probability of the argmax class, 5 bins with per-bin n shown.
   145	- `validNonNullYield` = valid forecasts on cases whose outcome is not `none` / total cases (renamed from "cost per useful
   146	  experiment"; it is a yield, not a cost). `costPerUseful` = total evaluation cost in simulated cell-steps (all arms,
   147	  nulls and failed attempts included) / count of cases whose outcome is non-null; null when the denominator is 0.
   148	- **Cross-seed effect stability** (renamed from "counterfactual agreement"; no "ceiling"): two complete pairs
   149	  `(treated_s, control_s)` and `(treated_s', control_s')` per qualification case; sign agreement reported. It measures
   150	  heterogeneity across seeds of the same family, not a predictor's accuracy limit. Seed variants share a family/split.
   151	- **Uncertainty unit** (finding 11): the family. Per-family aggregates, whole-family bootstrap with a stated resample
   152	  count/seed, paired differences between proposers on the same families. Intervals are computed only when the test split
   153	  has >= 10 families; otherwise only counts and per-family tables are shown ("descriptive"). Bin and class denominators
   154	  stated; missing-class handling stated.
   155	
   156	### 9. Outputs (finding 14)
   157	Under `experiments/prediction-envelope/v0/`:
   158	- canonical, byte-reproducible: `envelopes.jsonl`, `protocol.snapshot.json`, `recipes.jsonl` (config, seed, steps, family:
   159	  full replay inputs), `predictions.jsonl`, `outcomes.jsonl` (with envelope hash and protocol hash), `comparison.json`,
   160	  `comparison.md`, `report.html`, `canonical.sha256` listing hashes of exactly these files.
   161	- non-canonical: `meta.json` (git SHA, a source-closure hash over the tool files and packages used, rule/schema/metrics
   162	  versions, config hashes, commands, hardware, timing, no-model/no-GPU statement). Excluded from the reproducibility diff.
   163	Budget < 1.5 MB; if exceeded, commit `canonical.sha256` + recipes only and say which files were regenerated locally.
   164	
   165	### 10. Notebook surface
   166	`report.html` (static, uses `tools/lib/report-html`, no scripts, no external URLs, works from `file://`): per case an
   167	observation block (census sparkline, frame thumbnails), a hypothesis block (probs per proposer), a measured-outcome block
   168	(paired result), visibly distinct; failed predictions first; filter for invalid. Reason text escaped. No lab change.

     1	// Scoring (PLAN.md, section 8). Multiclass Brier over {up, down, none}: BS = (1/N) sum_i sum_c (p_ic/1000 - o_ic)^2, range 0..2.
     2	// The headline imputes abstentions and invalid/missing predictions with the frozen dev-split prior, so abstaining cannot beat the
     3	// prior; answered-only numbers are reported beside it. A calibrated majority baseline is legitimately strong and is not claimed
     4	// to be punished. The family is the unit of uncertainty.
     5	import { mulberry32 } from "@bl/metrics";
     6	import { CLASSES, type Direction3 } from "./envelope.ts";
     7	import { argmax, type Prediction, type Probs } from "./validate.ts";
     8	
     9	export interface ScoredCase {
    10	  caseId: string;
    11	  familyId: string;
    12	  outcome: Direction3;
    13	  /** null: abstained, invalid or missing. */
    14	  prediction: Prediction | null;
    15	  status: "answered" | "abstained" | "invalid" | "missing";
    16	}
    17	
    18	export const brierOf = (p: Probs, o: Direction3): number => {
    19	  let s = 0;
    20	  for (const c of CLASSES) {
    21	    const d = p[c] / 1000 - (c === o ? 1 : 0);
    22	    s += d * d;
    23	  }
    24	  return s;
    25	};
    26	
    27	export interface Reliability {
    28	  bin: string;
    29	  n: number;
    30	  meanStated: number | null;
    31	  hitRate: number | null;
    32	}
    33	export interface ProposerScore {
    34	  id: string;
    35	  n: number;
    36	  answered: number;
    37	  abstained: number;
    38	  invalid: number;
    39	  missing: number;
    40	  coverage: number;
    41	  abstentionRate: number;
    42	  /** Headline: abstained/invalid/missing imputed with the dev prior. */
    43	  brierImputed: number | null;
    44	  brierAnswered: number | null;
    45	  directionalAccuracy: number | null;
    46	  balancedAccuracy: number | null;
    47	  /** Classes with at least one answered case (the balanced accuracy's denominator). */
    48	  balancedClasses: number;
    49	  reliability: Reliability[];
    50	  validNonNullYield: number | null;
    51	}
    52	
    53	export function reliabilityTable(cases: readonly ScoredCase[]): Reliability[] {
    54	  const bins = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0].map(() => ({ n: 0, stated: 0, hit: 0 }));
    55	  for (const c of cases) {
    56	    if (c.status !== "answered" || !c.prediction?.probs) continue;
    57	    const a = argmax(c.prediction.probs);
    58	    const p = c.prediction.probs[a] / 1000;
    59	    const b = Math.min(4, Math.floor(p * 5));
    60	    bins[b].n++;
    61	    bins[b].stated += p;
    62	    if (a === c.outcome) bins[b].hit++;
    63	  }
    64	  return bins.map((b, i) => ({ bin: `${(i * 0.2).toFixed(1)}-${i === 4 ? "1.0" : ((i + 1) * 0.2).toFixed(1)}`, n: b.n, meanStated: b.n ? b.stated / b.n : null, hitRate: b.n ? b.hit / b.n : null }));
    65	}
    66	
    67	export function scoreProposer(id: string, cases: readonly ScoredCase[], devPrior: Probs, restrictTo?: ReadonlySet<string>): ProposerScore {
    68	  const cs = restrictTo ? cases.filter((c) => restrictTo.has(c.caseId)) : cases;
    69	  const n = cs.length;
    70	  const answered = cs.filter((c) => c.status === "answered");
    71	  let impSum = 0, ansSum = 0, hit = 0;
    72	  for (const c of cs) impSum += c.status === "answered" ? brierOf(c.prediction!.probs!, c.outcome) : brierOf(devPrior, c.outcome);
    73	  for (const c of answered) {
    74	    ansSum += brierOf(c.prediction!.probs!, c.outcome);
    75	    if (argmax(c.prediction!.probs!) === c.outcome) hit++;
    76	  }
    77	  let recSum = 0, recK = 0;
    78	  for (const k of CLASSES) {
    79	    const rows = answered.filter((c) => c.outcome === k);
    80	    if (!rows.length) continue;
    81	    recK++;
    82	    recSum += rows.filter((c) => argmax(c.prediction!.probs!) === k).length / rows.length;
    83	  }
    84	  return {
    85	    id,
    86	    n,
    87	    answered: answered.length,
    88	    abstained: cs.filter((c) => c.status === "abstained").length,
    89	    invalid: cs.filter((c) => c.status === "invalid").length,
    90	    missing: cs.filter((c) => c.status === "missing").length,
    91	    coverage: n ? answered.length / n : 0,
    92	    abstentionRate: n ? cs.filter((c) => c.status === "abstained").length / n : 0,
    93	    brierImputed: n ? impSum / n : null,
    94	    brierAnswered: answered.length ? ansSum / answered.length : null,
    95	    directionalAccuracy: answered.length ? hit / answered.length : null,
    96	    balancedAccuracy: recK ? recSum / recK : null,
    97	    balancedClasses: recK,
    98	    reliability: reliabilityTable(cs),
    99	    validNonNullYield: n ? answered.filter((c) => c.outcome !== "none").length / n : null,
   100	  };
   101	}
   102	
   103	/** Cases that every named comparator answered (the least-covering one's set when nested; else the intersection). */
   104	export function matchedCoverageSet(byProposer: Readonly<Record<string, readonly ScoredCase[]>>): Set<string> {
   105	  let acc: Set<string> | undefined;
   106	  for (const cs of Object.values(byProposer)) {
   107	    const a = new Set<string>(cs.filter((c) => c.status === "answered").map((c) => c.caseId));
   108	    const prev: Set<string> | undefined = acc;
   109	    acc = prev === undefined ? a : new Set<string>([...prev].filter((x) => a.has(x)));
   110	  }
   111	  return acc ?? new Set<string>();
   112	}
   113	
   114	/** Total evaluation cost (simulated cell-steps, every arm, null and failed attempts included) over non-null cases; null when there are none. */
   115	export function costPerUseful(totalCellSteps: number, outcomes: readonly Direction3[]): number | null {
   116	  const k = outcomes.filter((o) => o !== "none").length;
   117	  return k === 0 ? null : totalCellSteps / k;
   118	}
   119	
   120	/** Sign agreement of two effect vectors' labels (cross-seed effect stability); cases are paired by index. */
   121	export function signAgreement(a: readonly Direction3[], b: readonly Direction3[]): { n: number; agree: number; rate: number | null } {
   122	  if (a.length !== b.length) throw new Error("label vectors differ in length");
   123	  let agree = 0;
   124	  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) if (a[i] === b[i]) agree++;
   125	  return { n: a.length, agree, rate: a.length ? agree / a.length : null };
   126	}
   127	
   128	export interface Interval {
   129	  mean: number;
   130	  lo: number;
   131	  hi: number;
   132	}
   133	export const MIN_FAMILIES_FOR_INTERVALS = 10;
   134	
   135	/** Per-family mean of a per-case value. */
   136	export function familyMeans(rows: readonly { familyId: string; v: number }[]): Map<string, number> {
   137	  const acc = new Map<string, { s: number; n: number }>();
   138	  for (const r of rows) {
   139	    const a = acc.get(r.familyId) ?? { s: 0, n: 0 };
   140	    a.s += r.v;
   141	    a.n++;
   142	    acc.set(r.familyId, a);
   143	  }
   144	  return new Map([...acc].map(([k, a]) => [k, a.s / a.n]));
   145	}
   146	
   147	/** Whole-family bootstrap of the mean of per-family values; null below MIN_FAMILIES_FOR_INTERVALS families (descriptive only). */
   148	export function familyBootstrap(perFamily: ReadonlyMap<string, number>, resamples: number, seed: number): Interval | null {
   149	  const v = [...perFamily.keys()].sort().map((k) => perFamily.get(k)!);
   150	  if (v.length < MIN_FAMILIES_FOR_INTERVALS) return null;
   151	  const rng = mulberry32(seed);
   152	  const means: number[] = [];
   153	  for (let r = 0; r < resamples; r++) {
   154	    let s = 0;
   155	    for (let i = 0; i < v.length; i++) s += v[Math.floor(rng() * v.length)];
   156	    means.push(s / v.length);
   157	  }
   158	  means.sort((a, b) => a - b);
   159	  return { mean: v.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / v.length, lo: means[Math.floor(0.025 * resamples)], hi: means[Math.min(resamples - 1, Math.ceil(0.975 * resamples) - 1)] };
   160	}
   161	
   162	/** Paired per-family difference (a - b) of two per-family maps over the families both have. */
   163	export function pairedFamilyDiff(a: ReadonlyMap<string, number>, b: ReadonlyMap<string, number>): Map<string, number> {
   164	  const out = new Map<string, number>();
   165	  for (const [k, x] of a) if (b.has(k)) out.set(k, x - b.get(k)!);
   166	  return out;
   167	}
     1	// Whole-family splits and the leak checks (PLAN.md, section 7). A family is one source history with all its windows and seed
     2	// variants; a split never cuts through one. Ranking is by SHA-256 of salt + family id, so the assignment is deterministic and
     3	// independent of input order. A reserved `qualify` subset of dev is used for all qualification and tuning.
     4	import { sha256Text } from "../propose.ts";
     5	import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "./envelope.ts";
     6	
     7	export type Split = "dev" | "test";
     8	
     9	const rank = (salt: string, id: string): string => sha256Text(`${salt}|${id}`);
    10	
    11	export interface SplitPlan {
    12	  dev: string[];
    13	  test: string[];
    14	  qualify: string[];
    15	}
    16	
    17	/** First `nDev` families by hash rank are dev (the first `nQualify` of those are qualify), the rest test. Lists are in rank order. */
    18	export function assignSplits(familyIds: readonly string[], salt: string, nDev: number, nQualify: number): SplitPlan {
    19	  const uniq = [...new Set(familyIds)];
    20	  if (uniq.length !== familyIds.length) throw new Error("duplicate family ids");
    21	  if (nQualify > nDev || nDev >= uniq.length) throw new Error("need 0 <= nQualify <= nDev < number of families");
    22	  const ranked = uniq.map((id) => ({ id, h: rank(salt, id) })).sort((a, b) => (a.h < b.h ? -1 : a.h > b.h ? 1 : 0)).map((x) => x.id);
    23	  return { dev: ranked.slice(0, nDev), test: ranked.slice(nDev), qualify: ranked.slice(0, nQualify) };
    24	}
    25	
    26	/**
    27	 * Donor assignment for the shuffled-evidence control: a derangement of the families within one split (a cyclic shift of the hash
    28	 * ranking, so no family is its own donor and none crosses a split), frozen into the protocol before any scoring.
    29	 */
    30	export function derangement(familyIds: readonly string[], salt: string): Record<string, string> {
    31	  if (familyIds.length < 2) throw new Error("a derangement needs at least two families");
    32	  const ranked = familyIds.map((id) => ({ id, h: rank(`${salt}|donor`, id) })).sort((a, b) => (a.h < b.h ? -1 : a.h > b.h ? 1 : 0)).map((x) => x.id);
    33	  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
    34	  ranked.forEach((id, i) => (out[id] = ranked[(i + 1) % ranked.length]));
    35	  return out;
    36	}
    37	
    38	export function donorsFor(plan: SplitPlan, salt: string): Record<string, string> {
    39	  return { ...derangement(plan.dev, salt), ...derangement(plan.test, salt) };
    40	}
    41	
    42	export interface DonorCheck {
    43	  ok: boolean;
    44	  reason?: string;
    45	}
    46	/** A donor envelope is acceptable only from a different family and history, in the same split. */
    47	export function donorAllowed(env: Pick<PredictionEnvelope, "familyId" | "historyId">, donor: Pick<PredictionEnvelope, "familyId" | "historyId">, splitOf: (familyId: string) => Split | undefined): DonorCheck {
    48	  if (donor.familyId === env.familyId) return { ok: false, reason: "donor is from the same family" };
    49	  if (donor.historyId === env.historyId) return { ok: false, reason: "donor is another window of the same history" };
    50	  const a = splitOf(env.familyId), b = splitOf(donor.familyId);
    51	  if (a === undefined || b === undefined || a !== b) return { ok: false, reason: "donor is from another split" };
    52	  return { ok: true };
    53	}
    54	
    55	/** Returns the problems found (empty when clean): overlapping families, a family in two splits, bad donors, test envelopes sharing a census with a dev one. */
    56	export function assertNoLeak(plan: SplitPlan, donors: Record<string, string>, envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[]): string[] {
    57	  const errs: string[] = [];
    58	  const dev = new Set(plan.dev), test = new Set(plan.test);
    59	  for (const f of dev) if (test.has(f)) errs.push(`family ${f} is in both dev and test`);
    60	  for (const f of plan.qualify) if (!dev.has(f)) errs.push(`qualify family ${f} is not in dev`);
    61	  const splitOf = (f: string): Split | undefined => (dev.has(f) ? "dev" : test.has(f) ? "test" : undefined);
    62	  for (const [f, d] of Object.entries(donors)) {
    63	    if (f === d) errs.push(`family ${f} is its own donor`);
    64	    else if (splitOf(f) !== splitOf(d)) errs.push(`donor ${d} of ${f} is in another split`);
    65	  }
    66	  for (const f of [...dev, ...test]) if (donors[f] === undefined) errs.push(`family ${f} has no donor`);
    67	  const hist = new Map<string, string>();
    68	  for (const e of envelopes) {
    69	    if (splitOf(e.familyId) === undefined) errs.push(`envelope ${e.caseId} belongs to no split`);
    70	    const prev = hist.get(e.historyId);
    71	    if (prev !== undefined && prev !== e.familyId) errs.push(`history ${e.historyId} appears under two families`);
    72	    hist.set(e.historyId, e.familyId);
    73	  }
    74	  const devCensus = new Set<string>();
    75	  for (const e of envelopes) if (dev.has(e.familyId)) devCensus.add(JSON.stringify(e.census));
    76	  for (const e of envelopes) if (test.has(e.familyId) && devCensus.has(JSON.stringify(e.census))) errs.push(`test envelope ${e.caseId} carries a dev census`);
    77	  return errs;
    78	}

     1	// Deterministic baseline proposers (PLAN.md, section 4). A proposer takes only an envelope and returns a prediction (never
     2	// outcomes, never another case's task). Constants are frozen in protocol.json and passed in; nothing here reads a file or a clock.
     3	import { RULES, type Prediction as RulesPrediction } from "../propose.ts";
     4	import { PREDICTION_VERSION, taskKey, type CensusPoint, type Direction3, type PredictionEnvelope } from "./envelope.ts";
     5	import { donorAllowed, type Split } from "./splits.ts";

     8	export interface Proposer {
     9	  id: string;
    10	  /** baseline: a rule a model has to beat; control: a deliberately uninformed run; oracle: knows the mechanism, a dev diagnostic only. */
    11	  role: "baseline" | "control" | "oracle";
    12	  propose(env: PredictionEnvelope): Prediction;
    13	}
    14	
    15	export interface ProposerParams {
    16	  fixedRule: { lead: number };
    17	  numericTrend: { window: number; minRelPerMille: number; lead: number; mid: number; low: number };
    18	  v0Rules: { lead: number };
    19	  mechanismRule: { lead: number; lowDoseQuanta: number; scarceNutrient: number };
    20	}

    22	export function predictionOf(env: PredictionEnvelope, probs: Probs | null, reason: string): Prediction {
    23	  const base = { version: PREDICTION_VERSION, caseId: env.caseId, envelopeHash: env.envelopeHash, task: env.task, reason };
    24	  return probs ? { ...base, probs, abstain: false } : { ...base, abstain: true };
    25	}
    26	
    27	/** `lead` per-mille on `dir`, the rest split evenly between the other two (the first, in order up/down/none, takes any odd remainder). */
    28	export function leadProbs(dir: Direction3, lead: number): Probs {
    29	  const rest = 1000 - lead;
    30	  const half = Math.floor(rest / 2);
    31	  const out: Probs = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
    32	  const others = (["up", "down", "none"] as const).filter((c) => c !== dir);
    33	  out[dir] = lead;
    34	  out[others[0]] = rest - half;
    35	  out[others[1]] = half;
    36	  return out;
    37	}
    38	
    39	/** Largest-remainder rounding of non-negative weights to per-mille summing to exactly 1000. */
    40	export function toPerMille(w: Record<Direction3, number>): Probs {
    41	  const tot = w.up + w.down + w.none;
    42	  if (!(tot > 0)) throw new Error("weights must have a positive sum");
    43	  const cls = ["up", "down", "none"] as const;
    44	  const raw = cls.map((c) => (w[c] * 1000) / tot);
    45	  const fl = raw.map(Math.floor);
    46	  let left = 1000 - fl.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    47	  const order = raw.map((r, i) => ({ i, f: r - fl[i] })).sort((a, b) => b.f - a.f || a.i - b.i);
    48	  for (const o of order) {
    49	    if (left <= 0) break;
    50	    fl[o.i]++;
    51	    left--;
    52	  }
    53	  return { up: fl[0], down: fl[1], none: fl[2] };
    54	}

    56	export const fixedRule = (k: ProposerParams["fixedRule"]): Proposer => ({
    57	  id: "fixedRule",
    58	  role: "baseline",
    59	  propose(env) {
    60	    const kind = env.task.op.kind;
    61	    const dir: Direction3 = kind === "feed" ? "up" : kind === "lesion" || kind === "drain" ? "down" : "none";
    62	    return predictionOf(env, leadProbs(dir, k.lead), `fixed rule: ${kind} -> ${dir}`);
    63	  },
    64	});
    65	
    66	/** The census-only trend: slope of the primary readout over the last `window` samples, extrapolated over the horizon, as a share of the current level. Ignores frames and the task. */
    67	export function trendDirection(census: readonly CensusPoint[], horizon: number, k: ProposerParams["numericTrend"]): Direction3 {
    68	  if (census.length < k.window + 1) return "none";
    69	  const last = census[census.length - 1], first = census[census.length - 1 - k.window];
    70	  const span = last.step - first.step;
    71	  if (span <= 0) return "none";
    72	  // change over the horizon, per-mille of the current level, in integer arithmetic.
    73	  const delta = (last.mass - first.mass) * horizon * 1000;
    74	  const bar = k.minRelPerMille * Math.max(last.mass, 1) * span;
    75	  return delta >= bar ? "up" : delta <= -bar ? "down" : "none";
    76	}
    77	function trendProbs(dir: Direction3, k: ProposerParams["numericTrend"]): Probs {
    78	  if (dir === "none") return { up: k.low, down: k.low, none: 1000 - 2 * k.low };
    79	  const other: Direction3 = dir === "up" ? "down" : "up";
    80	  const out: Probs = { up: 0, down: 0, none: k.mid };
    81	  out[dir] = k.lead;
    82	  out[other] = 1000 - k.lead - k.mid;
    83	  return out;
    84	}
    85	export const numericTrend = (k: ProposerParams["numericTrend"]): Proposer => ({
    86	  id: "numericTrend",
    87	  role: "baseline",
    88	  propose(env) {
    89	    const d = trendDirection(env.census, env.task.horizon, k);
    90	    return predictionOf(env, trendProbs(d, k), `census trend -> ${d}`);
    91	  },
    92	});
    93	
    94	/**
    95	 * The shuffled-evidence control: the numeric-trend rule run on a donor family's census. The donor is assigned by the frozen
    96	 * derangement; a donor from the same family, the same history (a later window of it) or another split is refused (throws), so a
    97	 * control can never be handed the case's own future.
    98	 */
    99	export function shuffledEvidence(
   100	  k: ProposerParams["numericTrend"],
   101	  donors: Readonly<Record<string, string>>,
   102	  envelopeOf: (familyId: string) => PredictionEnvelope | undefined,
   103	  splitOf: (familyId: string) => Split | undefined,
   104	): Proposer {
   105	  return {
   106	    id: "shuffledEvidence",
   107	    role: "control",
   108	    propose(env) {
   109	      const donorFamily = donors[env.familyId];
   110	      const donor = donorFamily === undefined ? undefined : envelopeOf(donorFamily);
   111	      if (!donor) throw new Error(`no donor envelope for family ${env.familyId}`);
   112	      const ok = donorAllowed(env, donor, splitOf);
   113	      if (!ok.ok) throw new Error(`donor refused for ${env.caseId}: ${ok.reason}`);
   114	      const d = trendDirection(donor.census, env.task.horizon, k);
   115	      return predictionOf(env, trendProbs(d, k), `donor census trend -> ${d}`);
   116	    },
   117	  };
   118	}

   128	/** The v0 rule proposer's directional/replay prediction mapped into the schema: 600 on the stated direction, the remainder on none (explicit, versioned). */
   129	export const RULES_MAP_VERSION = 1;
   130	export function fromRulesProposal(env: PredictionEnvelope, pred: RulesPrediction, lead: number): Prediction {
   131	  const probs: Probs = pred.kind === "directional" && pred.direction ? { up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 - lead, [pred.direction]: lead } as Probs : { up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 };
   132	  return predictionOf(env, probs, `rules-v0 (map v${RULES_MAP_VERSION}): ${pred.kind}${pred.direction ? " " + pred.direction : ""}`);
   133	}
   134	export const v0Rules = (k: ProposerParams["v0Rules"]): Proposer => ({
   135	  id: "v0Rules",
   136	  role: "baseline",
   137	  propose(env) {
   138	    // The v0 rule table (tools/lib/propose.ts RULES): feed and drain rows from the nutrient-held signals, lesion from the standing-body signals, control otherwise.
   139	    const kind = env.task.op.kind;

   141	    const pred: RulesPrediction = row.action === "control" ? { kind: "replay", statement: "replays" } : { kind: "directional", statement: `${row.action}`, direction: row.direction };
   142	    return fromRulesProposal(env, pred, k.lead);

   146	/** Positive control, a dev diagnostic only: knows the fixture mechanisms (lesion centre occupancy from the frames, dose against the nutrient in the field). Never a baseline to beat. */
   147	export const mechanismRule = (k: ProposerParams["mechanismRule"]): Proposer => ({
   148	  id: "mechanismRule",
   149	  role: "oracle",
   150	  propose(env) {
   151	    const op = env.task.op;
   152	    const last = env.frames[env.frames.length - 1];
   153	    const cen = env.census[env.census.length - 1];
   154	    const block = (x: number, y: number): number => {
   155	      const w = env.config.tileW * env.config.tilesX;
   156	      const bs = Math.floor(w / last.side);
   157	      return last.mass[Math.floor(y / bs) * last.side + Math.floor(x / bs)];
   158	    };
   159	    let dir: Direction3 = "none";
   160	    if (op.kind === "lesion") dir = block(op.x!, op.y!) > 0 ? "down" : "none";
   161	    else if (op.kind === "feed") dir = (op.r! * op.r! * 3 * op.amount!) >= k.lowDoseQuanta ? "up" : "none";
   162	    else if (op.kind === "drain") dir = cen.freeNutrient >= k.scarceNutrient ? "down" : "none";
   163	    return predictionOf(env, leadProbs(dir, k.lead), `mechanism: ${op.kind} -> ${dir}`);
   164	  },
   165	});

   167	export const caseKey = (env: PredictionEnvelope): string => taskKey(env.task);
     1	// Readouts, version 1 (docs/prediction-envelope-2026-11-01/PLAN.md, section 5). Evaluated directly from a WorldState, with no tracker and no
     2	// census history. The primary world-level readout is `bodyMass`: total bound mass B+P. The plan named `livingCells` (cells whose
     3	// lineage word is non-zero, which a test cross-checks against @bl/metrics `census().livingCells`); the dev probe found it does not
     4	// move under a feed (effect 0 in every probed world, because a lineage persists on a cell while its mass grows), so no "up" outcome
     5	// was reachable, and the primary readout was changed to `bodyMass` before the freeze (EVIDENCE.md, "Deviations"). `livingCells`
     6	// and `freeNutrient` (total A in the field) remain as diagnostics. Each readout has its own unit; effects are never pooled.
     7	import { CH, cellCount, isLiving, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     8	
     9	export const READOUTS_VERSION = 1;
    10	export type ReadoutId = "bodyMass";
    11	export const PRIMARY_READOUT: ReadoutId = "bodyMass";
    12	export type DiagnosticId = "livingCells" | "freeNutrient";
    13	export const READOUT_UNITS: Readonly<Record<ReadoutId | DiagnosticId, string>> = Object.freeze({ bodyMass: "mass quanta (B+P)", livingCells: "cells", freeNutrient: "nutrient quanta" });
    14	
    15	export function livingCells(s: WorldState): number {
    16	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    17	  let k = 0;
    18	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (isLiving(s, i)) k++;
    19	  return k;
    20	}
    21	
    22	export function freeNutrient(s: WorldState): number {
    23	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    24	  let t = 0;
    25	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) t += s.cells[CH.A * n + i];
    26	  return t;
    27	}
    28	
    29	export function totalMass(s: WorldState): number {
    30	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
    31	  let t = 0;
    32	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) t += s.cells[CH.B * n + i] + s.cells[CH.P * n + i];
    33	  return t;
    34	}
    35	
    36	export function readout(s: WorldState, id: ReadoutId): number {
    37	  if (id === "bodyMass") return totalMass(s);
    38	  throw new Error(`unknown readout ${String(id)}`);
    39	}
     1	// A tool-local CPU intervention adapter (PLAN.md, section 6). `RefSim` is not an `InterventionSim`, and the runner's CPU adapter is
     2	// private, so this mirrors it on a deep clone of a WorldState: advance to a step, apply one scheduled operation with the reference
     3	// `applyLesion` / `applyFeed`, advance again. It logs each application (requested vs delivered matter) and books matter and
     4	// energy the way the runner does, so each arm can be checked: energy by `ledgerResidual == 0`, matter by delivered feed (a lesion
     5	// converts matter in place and moves none).
     6	import { clampLesionRadius, cloneState, ledgerResidual, totalsOf, FeedRefusedError, applyFeed, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     7	import { RefSim, applyLesion } from "@bl/sim-ref";
     8	import type { ScheduledIntervention } from "@bl/runner";
     9	
    10	export interface AppliedLog {
    11	  step: number;
    12	  kind: "lesion" | "feed";
    13	  requestedAmount: number | null;
    14	  deliveredMatter: number;
    15	  radius: number;
    16	  refused: string | null;
    17	}
    18	
    19	export class CpuInterventionAdapter {
    20	  readonly sim: RefSim;
    21	  readonly log: AppliedLog[] = [];
    22	  private fedMatter = 0n;
    23	  private fedEnergy = 0n;
    24	  private readonly startMatter: bigint;
    25	  private readonly startEnergy: bigint;
    26	
    27	  /** `source` is cloned; `startTotals` are the totals of the world's step-0 state (the ledger's baseline), not the fork's. */
    28	  constructor(source: WorldState, startTotals: { matter: bigint; energy: bigint }) {
    29	    this.sim = new RefSim(cloneState(source));
    30	    this.startMatter = startTotals.matter;
    31	    this.startEnergy = startTotals.energy;
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  get state(): WorldState {
    35	    return this.sim.state;
    36	  }
    37	
    38	  advanceTo(step: number): void {
    39	    const s = this.sim.state;
    40	    if (step < s.step) throw new Error(`cannot go back from step ${s.step} to ${step}`);
    41	    this.sim.run(step - s.step);
    42	  }
    43	
    44	  apply(e: ScheduledIntervention): AppliedLog {
    45	    if (e.step !== this.sim.state.step) throw new Error(`scheduled for step ${e.step}, world is at ${this.sim.state.step}`);
    46	    if (e.kind === "sun") throw new Error("sun is not on the prediction menu");
    47	    if (e.kind === "lesion") {
    48	      applyLesion(this.sim, e.x, e.y, e.r);
    49	      const line: AppliedLog = { step: e.step, kind: "lesion", requestedAmount: null, deliveredMatter: 0, radius: clampLesionRadius(this.sim.cfg, e.r), refused: null };
    50	      this.log.push(line);
    51	      return line;
    52	    }
    53	    try {
    54	      const res = applyFeed(this.sim.state, e.x, e.y, e.r, e.amount);
    55	      this.fedMatter += BigInt(res.matter);
    56	      this.fedEnergy += res.energy;
    57	      const line: AppliedLog = { step: e.step, kind: "feed", requestedAmount: e.amount, deliveredMatter: res.matter, radius: res.radius, refused: null };
    58	      this.log.push(line);
    59	      return line;
    60	    } catch (err) {
    61	      if (!(err instanceof FeedRefusedError)) throw err;
    62	      const line: AppliedLog = { step: e.step, kind: "feed", requestedAmount: e.amount, deliveredMatter: 0, radius: e.r, refused: err.message };
    63	      this.log.push(line);
    64	      return line;
    65	    }
    66	  }
    67	
    68	  /** Energy residual against the step-0 baseline moved by the fed energy: exactly 0n when the rules and the books agree. */
    69	  energyResidual(): bigint {
    70	    return ledgerResidual({ energy: this.startEnergy + this.fedEnergy }, this.sim.state);
    71	  }
    72	
    73	  /** Matter now minus (matter at step 0 + delivered feed): exactly 0n when matter is closed apart from the logged feeds. */
    74	  matterResidual(): bigint {
    75	    return totalsOf(this.sim.cfg, this.sim.state.cells).matter - (this.startMatter + this.fedMatter);
    76	  }
    77	}

     1	// Known-mechanism fixtures with outcomes from paired CPU replays (PLAN.md, section 6). Small worlds (32 x 32, kernelRadius 4) run
     2	// on `RefSim`. For one source history (a family) the generator
     3	//   1. runs the history twice to the fork step C: instrumented (census series and frame samples taken every few steps) and plain
     4	//      (state hashes only), and requires the two to agree on the full-state hash at every sampled step (observer neutrality);
     5	//   2. builds one envelope per mechanism case from the instrumented run (only data at steps <= C goes in);
     6	//   3. from the plain state at C runs a control arm (no operation) and, per case, a treated arm (advance to C+delta, apply, advance
     7	//      to C+H), every arm on its own deep clone, and records effect = readout(treated) - readout(control) at C+H.
     8	// Proposers see envelopes; only the scorer reads `effect`. Labels are derived from `effect` and the frozen `minEffect` at scoring time.
     9	import { CH, RULE_VERSION, PRESETS, buildWorld, cellCount, generalistGenome, isLiving, lowbias32, presetConfig, stateHash, totalsOf, worldW, cloneState, type Founder, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
    10	import { RefSim } from "@bl/sim-ref";
    11	import { sha256Text, canonical } from "../propose.ts";
    12	import { CpuInterventionAdapter } from "./adapter.ts";
    13	import { censusPoint, frameOf, sealEnvelope, standardMenu, type Direction3, type OpKind, type PredictionEnvelope, type Task, type TaskOp } from "./envelope.ts";
    14	import { freeNutrient, livingCells, totalMass, type DiagnosticId, type ReadoutId } from "./readouts.ts";
    15	import { taskSchedule } from "./validate.ts";

    17	export interface FamilySpec {
    18	  id: string;
    19	  seed: number;

    21	  nutrient: number;
    22	  founders: number;
    23	}
    24	export interface MechanismSpec {
    25	  id: string;
    26	  kind: OpKind;
    27	  where: "occupied" | "empty" | "none";
    28	  r?: number;
    29	  amount?: number;
    30	  /** The mechanism's expectation. Measured outcomes are never relabelled to match it; disagreements are reported. */
    31	  expected: Direction3;
    32	}

    34	  forkStep: number;

    40	export interface WorldSpec {
    41	  side: number;
    42	  kernelRadius: number;
    43	  founderRadius: number;
    44	  founderBiomass: number;
    45	  founderEnergy: number;
    46	}
    47	export interface FixtureProtocol {
    48	  world: WorldSpec;
    49	  window: WindowSpec;
    50	  menu: { rMax: number; amountMax: number };
    51	  mechanisms: MechanismSpec[];
    52	  seedVariantOffset: number;
    53	}

    55	export function makeWorld(p: FixtureProtocol, fam: FamilySpec, seed = fam.seed): WorldState {
    56	  const preset = PRESETS.find((x) => x.id === fam.light)!;
    57	  const cfg = presetConfig(preset, seed, { tileW: p.world.side, tileH: p.world.side, kernelRadius: p.world.kernelRadius });
    58	  const W = p.world.side;
    59	  const founders: Founder[] = [];
    60	  for (let i = 0; i < fam.founders; i++) {
    61	    const h = lowbias32(seed * 31 + i);
    62	    founders.push({ x: h % W, y: (h >>> 12) % W, radius: p.world.founderRadius, genome: generalistGenome(cfg.defaultMu, cfg.defaultSigma), biomass: p.world.founderBiomass, energy: p.world.founderEnergy });
    63	  }
    64	  return buildWorld(cfg, { nutrient: fam.nutrient, founders });
    65	}
    66	
    67	/** Cell index of the heaviest cell (B+P), ties to the lowest index. */
    68	export function heaviestCell(s: WorldState): { x: number; y: number } {
    69	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg), W = worldW(s.cfg);
    70	  let best = -1, bi = 0;
    71	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    72	    const m = s.cells[CH.B * n + i] + s.cells[CH.P * n + i];
    73	    if (m > best) {
    74	      best = m;
    75	      bi = i;
    76	    }
    77	  }
    78	  return { x: bi % W, y: Math.floor(bi / W) };
    79	}
    80	
    81	/** The cell farthest (wrapped squared distance) from every living cell; ties to the lowest index. If nothing lives, the world centre. */
    82	export function emptiestCell(s: WorldState): { x: number; y: number } {
    83	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg), W = worldW(s.cfg), H = n / W;
    84	  const live: number[] = [];
    85	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (isLiving(s, i)) live.push(i);
    86	  if (live.length === 0) return { x: W >> 1, y: H >> 1 };
    87	  let best = -1, bi = 0;
    88	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    89	    const x = i % W, y = Math.floor(i / W);
    90	    let d = Infinity;
    91	    for (const j of live) {
    92	      const dx = Math.min(Math.abs(x - (j % W)), W - Math.abs(x - (j % W)));
    93	      const dy = Math.min(Math.abs(y - Math.floor(j / W)), H - Math.abs(y - Math.floor(j / W)));
    94	      d = Math.min(d, dx * dx + dy * dy);
    95	    }
    96	    if (d > best) {
    97	      best = d;
    98	      bi = i;
    99	    }
   100	  }
   101	  return { x: bi % W, y: Math.floor(bi / W) };
   102	}
   103	
   104	export function taskOpFor(m: MechanismSpec, at: { x: number; y: number } | null): TaskOp {
   105	  if (m.kind === "none") return { kind: "none" };
   106	  const x = at!.x, y = at!.y, r = m.r!;
   107	  return m.kind === "lesion" ? { kind: "lesion", x, y, r } : { kind: m.kind, x, y, r, amount: m.amount! };
   108	}

   110	export interface ArmRecord {
   111	  caseId: string;
   112	  mechanismId: string;
   113	  effect: Record<ReadoutId | DiagnosticId, number>;
   114	  treated: Record<ReadoutId | DiagnosticId, number>;
   115	  control: Record<ReadoutId | DiagnosticId, number>;
   116	  /** Hash of the treated arm's end state; for the sham equals the control's. */
   117	  treatedHash: string;
   118	  applied: { kind: string; deliveredMatter: number; refused: string | null; radius: number }[];
   119	  energyResidual: string;
   120	  matterResidual: string;
   121	}
   122	export interface FamilyResult {
   123	  familyId: string;
   124	  historyId: string;
   125	  seed: number;
   126	  envelopes: PredictionEnvelope[];
   127	  arms: ArmRecord[];
   128	  controlHash: string;
   129	  /** Hashes compared between instrumented and plain history runs, and how many sampled steps they covered. */
   130	  neutralityChecks: number;
   131	  /** Simulated cell-steps spent on this family, every arm and check included. */
   132	  cellSteps: number;
   133	}
   134	
   135	export const caseIdOf = (familyId: string, mechanismId: string): string => "c" + sha256Text(`${familyId}|${mechanismId}`).slice(0, 12);
   136	
   137	/** Runs one family. `variantSeed` replaces the family's seed (for the cross-seed stability check); the family id stays. */
   138	export function runFamily(p: FixtureProtocol, fam: FamilySpec, variantSeed?: number): FamilyResult {
   139	  const seed = variantSeed ?? fam.seed;
   140	  const { forkStep: C, delta, horizon: H, censusEvery, frameSteps } = p.window;
   141	  const n = cellCount(makeWorld(p, fam, seed).cfg);
   142	  let cellSteps = 0;
   143	  const world0 = makeWorld(p, fam, seed);
   144	  const startTotals = totalsOf(world0.cfg, world0.cells);
   145	
   146	  // The sampled steps: every census step, plus the frame steps (all <= C).
   147	  const sampled = [...new Set([...Array.from({ length: Math.floor(C / censusEvery) }, (_, i) => (i + 1) * censusEvery), ...frameSteps, C])].sort((a, b) => a - b);
   148	
   149	  // Instrumented history.
   150	  const inst = new RefSim(cloneState(world0));
   151	  const census = [censusPoint(inst.state, livingCells(inst.state), freeNutrient(inst.state), totalMass(inst.state))];
   152	  const frames = frameSteps.includes(0) ? [frameOf(inst.state)] : [];
   153	  const instHash: string[] = [];
   154	  for (const t of sampled) {
   155	    inst.run(t - inst.state.step);
   156	    if (t % censusEvery === 0) census.push(censusPoint(inst.state, livingCells(inst.state), freeNutrient(inst.state), totalMass(inst.state)));
   157	    if (frameSteps.includes(t)) frames.push(frameOf(inst.state));
   158	    instHash.push(stateHash(inst.state));
   159	  }
   160	  cellSteps += C * n;
   161	  // Plain history: hashes only.
   162	  const plain = new RefSim(cloneState(world0));
   163	  const plainHash: string[] = [];
   164	  for (const t of sampled) {
   165	    plain.run(t - plain.state.step);
   166	    plainHash.push(stateHash(plain.state));
   167	  }
   168	  cellSteps += C * n;
   169	  for (let i = 0; i < sampled.length; i++) if (instHash[i] !== plainHash[i]) throw new Error(`observer neutrality failed for ${fam.id} at step ${sampled[i]}`);
   170	  const forkState = plain.state;
   171	
   172	  const historyId = `${fam.id}:s${seed}`;
   173	  // Control arm.
   174	  const ctl = new CpuInterventionAdapter(forkState, startTotals);
   175	  ctl.advanceTo(C + H);
   176	  cellSteps += H * n;
   177	  checkBooks(ctl, `${fam.id} control`);
   178	  const control = { bodyMass: totalMass(ctl.state), livingCells: livingCells(ctl.state), freeNutrient: freeNutrient(ctl.state) };
   179	  const controlHash = stateHash(ctl.state);
   180	
   181	  const occupied = heaviestCell(forkState), empty = emptiestCell(forkState);
   182	  const envelopes: PredictionEnvelope[] = [];
   183	  const arms: ArmRecord[] = [];
   184	  for (const m of p.mechanisms) {
   185	    const at = m.where === "occupied" ? occupied : m.where === "empty" ? empty : null;
   186	    const op = taskOpFor(m, at);
   187	    const task: Task = { subject: "world", op, delta, readout: "bodyMass", forkStep: C, horizon: H };
   188	    const env = sealEnvelope({
   189	      version: 1,
   190	      caseId: caseIdOf(fam.id, m.id),
   191	      historyId,
   192	      familyId: fam.id,
   193	      ruleVersion: RULE_VERSION,
   194	      config: inst.cfg,
   195	      census,
   196	      frames,
   197	      menu: standardMenu(inst.cfg, delta + 50, p.menu.amountMax, p.menu.rMax),
   198	      task,
   199	    });
   200	    const ts = taskSchedule(env);
   201	    if (ts.errors.length) throw new Error(`fixture ${env.caseId} (${m.id}) is not a valid task: ${ts.errors.join("; ")}`);
   202	    envelopes.push(env);
   203	
   204	    const arm = new CpuInterventionAdapter(forkState, startTotals);
   205	    if (ts.schedule) {
   206	      arm.advanceTo(ts.schedule[0].step);
   207	      arm.apply(ts.schedule[0]);
   208	    }
   209	    arm.advanceTo(C + H);
   210	    cellSteps += H * n;
   211	    checkBooks(arm, `${fam.id} ${m.id}`);
   212	    const treated = { bodyMass: totalMass(arm.state), livingCells: livingCells(arm.state), freeNutrient: freeNutrient(arm.state) };
   213	    const treatedHash = stateHash(arm.state);
   214	    if (m.kind === "none" && treatedHash !== controlHash) throw new Error(`sham twin differs from control for ${fam.id}`);
   215	    arms.push({
   216	      caseId: env.caseId,
   217	      mechanismId: m.id,
   218	      effect: { bodyMass: treated.bodyMass - control.bodyMass, livingCells: treated.livingCells - control.livingCells, freeNutrient: treated.freeNutrient - control.freeNutrient },
   219	      treated,
   220	      control,
   221	      treatedHash,
   222	      applied: arm.log.map((l) => ({ kind: l.kind, deliveredMatter: l.deliveredMatter, refused: l.refused, radius: l.radius })),
   223	      energyResidual: arm.energyResidual().toString(),
   224	      matterResidual: arm.matterResidual().toString(),
   225	    });
   226	  }
   227	  return { familyId: fam.id, historyId, seed, envelopes, arms, controlHash, neutralityChecks: sampled.length, cellSteps };
   228	}
   229	
   230	function checkBooks(a: CpuInterventionAdapter, what: string): void {
   231	  if (a.energyResidual() !== 0n) throw new Error(`${what}: energy ledger residual ${a.energyResidual()}`);
   232	  if (a.matterResidual() !== 0n) throw new Error(`${what}: matter residual ${a.matterResidual()}`);
   233	}
   234	
   235	/** Label of an effect against a minimum effect in the readout's own unit. */
   236	export function labelOf(effect: number, minEffect: number): Direction3 {
   237	  return effect >= minEffect ? "up" : effect <= -minEffect ? "down" : "none";
   238	}
   239	
   240	export const recipeOf = (p: FixtureProtocol, fam: FamilySpec, seed: number): Record<string, unknown> => ({ family: fam, seed, world: p.world, window: p.window, menu: p.menu, mechanisms: p.mechanisms });
   241	export const recipeHash = (r: unknown): string => sha256Text(canonical(r));
     1	// The prediction-envelope harness CLI (docs/prediction-envelope-2026-11-01/PLAN.md). Deno, native CPU only: no GPU, no model call,
     2	// no network. Observer and tooling only; nothing here changes the dynamics.
     3	//
     4	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts protocol                       write the draft experiments/prediction-envelope/protocol.json (never overwrites)
     5	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts run --stage dev --out DIR      dev families only: DIR/dev/qualification.json (raw effects, tallies, stability)
     6	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts freeze --min-effect N [--out DIR]   set minEffect and devPrior from DIR/dev/qualification.json, write the freeze hash
     7	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts run --stage confirm --out DIR  all families; refuses unless the protocol hash matches protocol.freeze.sha256
     8	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts run --stage smoke --protocol P --out DIR   a small protocol, no freeze check (output marked SMOKE in meta.json)
     9	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts validate FILE [--envelopes F]  check a predictions file against its envelopes (wrapped {proposer, prediction} or bare lines)
    10	//   deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts report --out DIR               re-render report.html from the files in DIR
    11	import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
    12	import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
    13	import { cpus, platform, arch } from "node:os";
    14	import { dirname, join, resolve } from "node:path";
    15	import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
    16	import { canonical, sha256Text } from "./lib/propose.ts";
    17	import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "./lib/prediction/envelope.ts";
    18	import { CANONICAL_FILES, buildOutputs } from "./lib/prediction/output.ts";
    19	import { RUN_VERSIONS, confirmRefusal, draftProtocol, generate, priorFromEffects, protocolHash, qualificationReport, splitPlanOf, type Protocol } from "./lib/prediction/run.ts";
    20	import { validatePredictionFile } from "./lib/prediction/validate.ts";

    22	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
    23	const root = resolve(here, "..");
    24	const EXP = join(root, "experiments/prediction-envelope");
    25	const PROTOCOL = join(EXP, "protocol.json");
    26	const FREEZE = join(EXP, "protocol.freeze.sha256");

    28	function args(argv: string[]): { cmd: string; pos: string[]; opt: Record<string, string> } {
    29	  const [cmd = "", ...rest] = argv;
    30	  const pos: string[] = [], opt: Record<string, string> = {};
    31	  for (let i = 0; i < rest.length; i++) {
    32	    if (rest[i].startsWith("--")) {
    33	      opt[rest[i].slice(2)] = rest[i + 1] ?? "";
    34	      i++;
    35	    } else pos.push(rest[i]);
    36	  }
    37	  return { cmd, pos, opt };
    38	}
    39	const readProtocol = (path: string): Protocol => JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8")) as Protocol;
    40	const writeText = (path: string, text: string): void => {
    41	  mkdirSync(dirname(path), { recursive: true });
    42	  writeFileSync(path, text);
    43	};
    44	function sh(cmd: string, a: string[]): string {
    45	  try {
    46	    return execFileSync(cmd, a, { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8", stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "ignore"] }).trim();
    47	  } catch {
    48	    return "unknown";
    49	  }
    50	}
    51	function sourceClosureHash(): string {
    52	  const files = [
    53	    "tools/predict.ts",
    54	    "tools/lib/propose.ts",
    55	    ...["adapter", "envelope", "fixtures", "model-seam", "output", "proposers", "readouts", "report-html", "run", "score", "splits", "validate"].map((n) => `tools/lib/prediction/${n}.ts`),
    56	    "packages/sim-ref/src/step.ts",
    57	    "packages/schema/src/feed.ts",
    58	    "packages/schema/src/config.ts",
    59	  ];
    60	  return sha256Text(files.map((f) => `${f}\n${sha256Text(readFileSync(join(root, f), "utf8"))}`).join("\n"));
    61	}
    62	const readJsonl = <T>(path: string): T[] => readFileSync(path, "utf8").split("\n").filter((l) => l.trim() !== "").map((l) => JSON.parse(l) as T);
    63	
    64	function main(): number {
    65	  const { cmd, pos, opt } = args(Deno.args);
    66	  if (cmd === "protocol") {
    67	    if (existsSync(PROTOCOL)) {
    68	      console.error(`${PROTOCOL} exists; not overwriting`);
    69	      return 1;
    70	    }
    71	    writeText(PROTOCOL, JSON.stringify(draftProtocol(), null, 2) + "\n");
    72	    console.log(`wrote ${PROTOCOL}`);
    73	    return 0;
    74	  }
    75	  if (cmd === "run") {
    76	    const stage = opt.stage ?? "";
    77	    const out = resolve(opt.out ?? "");
    78	    if (!opt.out) throw new Error("--out is required");
    79	    const protoPath = stage === "smoke" ? resolve(opt.protocol ?? "") : PROTOCOL;
    80	    const p = readProtocol(protoPath);
    81	    const t0 = Date.now();
    82	    if (stage === "dev") {
    83	      if (p.frozen) throw new Error("the protocol is already frozen; the dev stage is for qualification before the freeze");
    84	      const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
    85	      const gen = generate(p, plan.dev, true);
    86	      writeText(join(out, "dev/qualification.json"), JSON.stringify(qualificationReport(p, gen), null, 1) + "\n");
    87	      console.log(`dev: ${gen.results.length} families, ${gen.variants.length} seed variants, ${Date.now() - t0} ms -> ${join(out, "dev/qualification.json")}`);
    88	      return 0;
    89	    }
    90	    if (stage === "confirm" || stage === "smoke") {
    91	      if (stage === "confirm") {
    92	        const refusal = confirmRefusal(p, existsSync(FREEZE) ? readFileSync(FREEZE, "utf8") : null);
    93	        if (refusal) throw new Error(`refusing to confirm: ${refusal}`);
    94	      }
    95	      const gen = generate(p, p.families.map((f) => f.id), true);
    96	      const built = buildOutputs(p, gen);
    97	      for (const [name, text] of Object.entries(built.files)) writeText(join(out, name), text);
    98	      const meta = {
    99	        stage,
   100	        status: stage === "smoke" ? "SMOKE" : "CONFIRM",
   101	        gitSha: sh("git", ["rev-parse", "HEAD"]),
   102	        gitDirty: sh("git", ["status", "--porcelain", "--", "tools", "packages", "experiments/prediction-envelope"]) !== "",
   103	        sourceClosureSha256: sourceClosureHash(),
   104	        versions: RUN_VERSIONS,
   105	        protocolSha256: protocolHash(p),
   106	        command: `deno run -A --no-lock tools/predict.ts ${Deno.args.join(" ")}`,
   107	        hardware: { cpu: cpus()[0]?.model ?? "unknown", cores: cpus().length, platform: platform(), arch: arch() },
   108	        wallMs: Date.now() - t0,
   109	        cellSteps: built.comparison.validNonNullYieldAndCost.totalCellSteps,
   110	        statement: "CPU only (sim-ref). No model call, no GPU, no network, no paid compute.",
   111	      };
   112	      writeText(join(out, "meta.json"), JSON.stringify(meta, null, 2) + "\n");
   113	      console.log(`${stage}: ${gen.results.length} families, ${built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].split("\n").length - 1} cases, ${Date.now() - t0} ms -> ${out}`);
   114	      return 0;
   115	    }
   116	    throw new Error(`unknown --stage ${JSON.stringify(stage)} (dev, confirm, smoke)`);
   117	  }
   118	  if (cmd === "freeze") {
   119	    const out = resolve(opt.out ?? join(EXP, "v0"));
   120	    const min = Number(opt["min-effect"]);
   121	    if (!Number.isFinite(min) || min <= 0) throw new Error("--min-effect must be a positive number");
   122	    const p = readProtocol(PROTOCOL);
   123	    if (p.frozen) throw new Error("already frozen");
   124	    const q = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(out, "dev/qualification.json"), "utf8")) as { protocolHash: string; effects: { bodyMass: number }[] };
   125	    if (q.protocolHash !== protocolHash(p)) throw new Error("dev/qualification.json was made with a different draft protocol");
   126	    p.minEffect = min;
   127	    p.devPrior = priorFromEffects(q.effects.map((e) => e.bodyMass), min);
   128	    p.frozen = true;
   129	    writeText(PROTOCOL, JSON.stringify(p, null, 2) + "\n");
   130	    writeText(FREEZE, protocolHash(p) + "\n");
   131	    console.log(`frozen: minEffect ${min}, devPrior ${JSON.stringify(p.devPrior)}, hash ${protocolHash(p)}`);
   132	    return 0;
   133	  }
   134	  if (cmd === "validate") {
   135	    const file = resolve(pos[0] ?? "");
   136	    const envPath = resolve(opt.envelopes ?? join(dirname(file), "envelopes.jsonl"));
   137	    const envelopes = readJsonl<PredictionEnvelope>(envPath);
   138	    const lines = readFileSync(file, "utf8").split("\n").filter((l) => l.trim() !== "");
   139	    const groups = new Map<string, string[]>();
   140	    for (const l of lines) {
   141	      let proposer = "(bare)", body = l;
   142	      try {
   143	        const o = JSON.parse(l) as { proposer?: unknown; prediction?: unknown };
   144	        if (o && typeof o === "object" && typeof o.proposer === "string" && "prediction" in o) {
   145	          proposer = o.proposer;
   146	          body = JSON.stringify(o.prediction);
   147	        }
   148	      } catch {
   149	        /* the validator counts it */
   150	      }
   151	      (groups.get(proposer) ?? groups.set(proposer, []).get(proposer)!).push(body);
   152	    }
   153	    let bad = 0;
   154	    for (const [id, ls] of groups) {
   155	      const r = validatePredictionFile(ls, envelopes);
   156	      bad += r.invalid + r.missing.length + r.extra.length;
   157	      console.log(`${id}: ${ls.length} lines, invalid ${r.invalid}, missing ${r.missing.length}, extra ${r.extra.length}, duplicates ${r.duplicates.length}`);
   158	    }
   159	    return bad === 0 ? 0 : 1;
   160	  }
   161	  if (cmd === "report") {
   162	    // Re-rendering needs the generated results only for the comparison, which is stored; the report is rebuilt from the files.
   163	    throw new Error("report: the confirm stage writes report.html; re-run it to regenerate (the report is a pure function of the canonical files)");
   164	  }
   165	  console.error("usage: predict.ts protocol | run --stage dev|confirm|smoke --out DIR | freeze --min-effect N | validate FILE | report --out DIR");
   166	  return 2;
   167	}
   168	
   169	try {
   170	  Deno.exit(main());
   171	} catch (e) {
   172	  console.error((e as Error).message);
   173	  Deno.exit(1);
   174	}
   175	void CANONICAL_FILES;
   176	void canonical;

    37	/** The confirm stage's gate: the protocol must be frozen and its hash must equal the recorded freeze hash. Returns the refusal, or null. */
    38	export function confirmRefusal(p: Protocol, freezeText: string | null): string | null {
    39	  if (!p.frozen || p.minEffect === null || p.devPrior === null) return "protocol.json is not frozen (run the dev stage, then freeze)";
    40	  if (freezeText === null) return "protocol.freeze.sha256 is missing";
    41	  if (freezeText.trim() !== protocolHash(p)) return `protocol hash ${protocolHash(p)} does not match the frozen ${freezeText.trim()}`;
    42	  return null;

    92	export interface Generated {

    98	export function generate(p: Protocol, familyIds: readonly string[], includeVariants: boolean): Generated {
    99	  const byId = new Map(p.families.map((f) => [f.id, f]));
   100	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   101	  const results: FamilyResult[] = [];
   102	  const variants: FamilyResult[] = [];
   103	  for (const id of familyIds) {
   104	    const fam = byId.get(id);
   105	    if (!fam) throw new Error(`unknown family ${id}`);
   106	    const r = runFamily(p, fam);
   107	    if (r.cellSteps > p.caps.maxCellStepsPerFamily) throw new Error(`family ${id} used ${r.cellSteps} cell-steps, over the cap ${p.caps.maxCellStepsPerFamily}`);
   108	    results.push(r);
   109	    if (includeVariants && plan.qualify.includes(id)) variants.push(runFamily(p, fam, fam.seed + p.seedVariantOffset));
   110	  }
   111	  return { results, variants };
   112	}

   114	export interface CaseRow {

   128	export function caseRows(p: Protocol, g: readonly FamilyResult[]): CaseRow[] {
   129	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   130	  const rows: CaseRow[] = [];
   131	  for (const r of g) {
   132	    const split: Split = plan.dev.includes(r.familyId) ? "dev" : "test";
   133	    for (const a of r.arms) {
   134	      const env = r.envelopes.find((e) => e.caseId === a.caseId)!;
   135	      const m = p.mechanisms.find((x) => x.id === a.mechanismId)!;
   136	      rows.push({
   137	        caseId: a.caseId,
   138	        familyId: r.familyId,
   139	        split,
   140	        mechanismId: a.mechanismId,
   141	        expected: m.expected,
   142	        envelopeHash: env.envelopeHash,
   143	        effect: { bodyMass: a.effect.bodyMass, livingCells: a.effect.livingCells, freeNutrient: a.effect.freeNutrient },
   144	        before: env.census[env.census.length - 1].mass,
   145	        treated: a.treated.bodyMass,
   146	        control: a.control.bodyMass,
   147	        treatedHash: a.treatedHash,
   148	      });
   149	    }
   150	  }
   151	  return rows;
   152	}

   154	/** Prespecified: a label that changes when the threshold is scaled by 3/4 or 5/4 is measurement-limited (kept in every denominator). */
   155	export function measurementLimited(effect: number, minEffect: number): boolean {
   156	  const l = labelOf(effect, minEffect);
   157	  return labelOf(effect, minEffect * 0.75) !== l || labelOf(effect, minEffect * 1.25) !== l;
   158	}
   159	
   160	/** The dev qualification report: raw effects, label tallies at each candidate threshold, expected vs measured, cross-seed stability. */
   161	export function qualificationReport(p: Protocol, dev: Generated) {
   162	  const rows = caseRows(p, dev.results);
   163	  const tallies = p.minEffectCandidates.map((t) => {
   164	    const perMech = p.mechanisms.map((m) => {
   165	      const rs = rows.filter((r) => r.mechanismId === m.id);
   166	      const count = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
   167	      for (const r of rs) count[labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, t)]++;
   168	      return { mechanism: m.id, expected: m.expected, ...count, agreeWithExpected: count[m.expected] };
   169	    });
   170	    return { minEffect: t, perMechanism: perMech };
   171	  });
   172	  const variantRows = caseRows(p, dev.variants);
   173	  const stability = p.minEffectCandidates.map((t) => {
   174	    const a: Direction3[] = [], b: Direction3[] = [];
   175	    for (const v of variantRows) {
   176	      const base = rows.find((r) => r.caseId === v.caseId);
   177	      if (!base) continue;
   178	      a.push(labelOf(base.effect.bodyMass, t));
   179	      b.push(labelOf(v.effect.bodyMass, t));
   180	    }
   181	    return { minEffect: t, ...signAgreement(a, b) };
   182	  });
   183	  return {
   184	    protocolHash: protocolHash(p),
   185	    note: "measured labels are never relabelled to match expectations; disagreements are reported",
   186	    effects: rows.map((r) => ({ caseId: r.caseId, familyId: r.familyId, mechanism: r.mechanismId, bodyMass: r.effect.bodyMass, livingCells: r.effect.livingCells, freeNutrient: r.effect.freeNutrient })),
   187	    variantEffects: variantRows.map((r) => ({ caseId: r.caseId, mechanism: r.mechanismId, bodyMass: r.effect.bodyMass })),
   188	    tallies,
   189	    crossSeedEffectStability: stability,
   190	    neutralityChecks: dev.results.reduce((s, r) => s + r.neutralityChecks, 0),
   191	    cellSteps: dev.results.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cellSteps, 0) + dev.variants.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cellSteps, 0),
   192	  };
   193	}
   194	
   195	/** Class frequencies of a list of effects at a threshold, as per-mille summing to exactly 1000 (largest remainder; ties to the earlier of up, down, none). */
   196	export function priorFromEffects(effects: readonly number[], minEffect: number): Probs {
   197	  const c = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
   198	  for (const e of effects) c[labelOf(e, minEffect)]++;
   199	  const n = effects.length;
   200	  if (n === 0) throw new Error("no dev effects");
   201	  const raw = CLASSES.map((k) => (c[k] * 1000) / n);
   202	  const fl = raw.map(Math.floor);
   203	  let left = 1000 - fl.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
   204	  const order = raw.map((r, i) => ({ i, f: r - fl[i] })).sort((a, b) => b.f - a.f || a.i - b.i);
   205	  for (const o of order) {
   206	    if (left <= 0) break;
   207	    fl[o.i]++;
   208	    left--;
   209	  }
   210	  return { up: fl[0], down: fl[1], none: fl[2] };
   211	}
   212	export const devPriorAt = (p: Protocol, dev: Generated, minEffect: number): Probs => priorFromEffects(caseRows(p, dev.results).map((r) => r.effect.bodyMass), minEffect);
   213	
   214	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215	// Proposers and evaluation.
   216	
   217	export function buildProposers(p: Protocol, envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[]): Proposer[] {
   218	  if (!p.devPrior) throw new Error("protocol has no devPrior: freeze it first");
   219	  const plan = splitPlanOf(p);
   220	  const splitOf = (f: string): Split | undefined => (plan.dev.includes(f) ? "dev" : plan.test.includes(f) ? "test" : undefined);
   221	  const first = new Map<string, PredictionEnvelope>();
   222	  for (const e of envelopes) if (!first.has(e.familyId)) first.set(e.familyId, e);
   223	  return [
   224	    priorMajority(p.devPrior),
   225	    alwaysNone(),
   226	    fixedRule(p.proposers.fixedRule),
   227	    numericTrend(p.proposers.numericTrend),
   228	    v0Rules(p.proposers.v0Rules),
   229	    shuffledEvidence(p.proposers.numericTrend, p.donors, (f) => first.get(f), splitOf),
   230	    abstainAll(),
   231	    mechanismRule(p.proposers.mechanismRule),
   232	  ];
   233	}

   237	export interface PredictionLine {

   242	export function runProposers(proposers: readonly Proposer[], envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[]): PredictionLine[] {
   243	  const out: PredictionLine[] = [];
   244	  for (const pr of proposers) for (const e of envelopes) out.push({ proposer: pr.id, prediction: pr.propose(structuredClone(e)) });
   245	  return out;
   246	}
   247	
   248	export function scoredCases(lines: readonly PredictionLine[], proposerId: string, envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[], rows: readonly CaseRow[], minEffect: number): ScoredCase[] {
   249	  const mine = lines.filter((l) => l.proposer === proposerId).map((l) => JSON.stringify(l.prediction));
   250	  const check = validatePredictionFile(mine, envelopes);
   251	  const byRow = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.caseId, r]));
   252	  const out: ScoredCase[] = [];
   253	  for (const e of envelopes) {
   254	    const row = byRow.get(e.caseId);
   255	    if (!row) continue;
   256	    const got = check.byCase.get(e.caseId);
   257	    const prediction = got?.prediction ?? null;
   258	    const status: ScoredCase["status"] = !got ? "missing" : !prediction ? "invalid" : prediction.abstain ? "abstained" : "answered";
   259	    out.push({ caseId: e.caseId, familyId: e.familyId, outcome: labelOf(row.effect.bodyMass, minEffect), prediction: status === "answered" ? prediction : null, status });
   260	  }
   261	  return out;
   262	}
   263	
   264	export interface TableRow extends ProposerScore {
   265	  role: string;
   266	  brierAtMatchedCoverage: number | null;
   267	  accuracyAtMatchedCoverage: number | null;
   268	}
   269	export interface SplitTable {
   270	  split: string;
   271	  nCases: number;
   272	  nFamilies: number;
   273	  classCounts: Record<Direction3, number>;
   274	  meanAbsEffect: Record<string, number | null>;
   275	  rows: TableRow[];
   276	}
   277	
   278	export function tableFor(p: Protocol, name: string, proposers: readonly Proposer[], lines: readonly PredictionLine[], envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[], rows: readonly CaseRow[], pick: (r: CaseRow) => boolean): SplitTable {
   279	  const sel = rows.filter(pick);
   280	  const ids = new Set(sel.map((r) => r.caseId));
   281	  const envs = envelopes.filter((e) => ids.has(e.caseId));
   282	  const prior = p.devPrior!;
   283	  const minEffect = p.minEffect!;
   284	  const byP: Record<string, ScoredCase[]> = {};
   285	  for (const pr of proposers) byP[pr.id] = scoredCases(lines, pr.id, envs, sel, minEffect);
   286	  // The coverage floor (a proposer that answers nothing) is left out of the matched set, else it would empty it.
   287	  const answering = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(byP).filter(([, cs]) => cs.some((c) => c.status === "answered")));
   288	  const matched = matchedCoverageSet(answering);
   289	  const classCounts: Record<Direction3, number> = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
   290	  for (const r of sel) classCounts[labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect)]++;
   291	  const mean = (f: (r: CaseRow) => number): number | null => (sel.length ? sel.reduce((s, r) => s + Math.abs(f(r)), 0) / sel.length : null);
   292	  return {
   293	    split: name,
   294	    nCases: sel.length,
   295	    nFamilies: new Set(sel.map((r) => r.familyId)).size,
   296	    classCounts,
   297	    meanAbsEffect: { [`bodyMass (${READOUT_UNITS.bodyMass})`]: mean((r) => r.effect.bodyMass), [`livingCells (${READOUT_UNITS.livingCells}, diagnostic)`]: mean((r) => r.effect.livingCells), [`freeNutrient (${READOUT_UNITS.freeNutrient}, diagnostic)`]: mean((r) => r.effect.freeNutrient) },
   298	    rows: proposers.map((pr) => {
   299	      const s = scoreProposer(pr.id, byP[pr.id], prior);
   300	      const m = matched.size ? scoreProposer(pr.id, byP[pr.id], prior, matched) : null;
   301	      return { ...s, role: pr.role, brierAtMatchedCoverage: m?.brierAnswered ?? null, accuracyAtMatchedCoverage: m?.directionalAccuracy ?? null };
   302	    }),
   303	  };
   304	}
   305	
   306	export interface Comparison {
   307	  protocolHash: string;
   308	  noModelStatement: string;
   309	  strongestBaselineOnDev: string;
   310	  dev: SplitTable;
   311	  test: SplitTable;
   312	  testExcludingMeasurementLimited: SplitTable;
   313	  measurementLimitedCases: number;
   314	  perFamily: Record<string, { intervals: boolean; brierImputed: Record<string, Interval | null>; pairedVsStrongest: Record<string, Interval | null> }>;
   315	  mechanismAudit: { mechanism: string; expected: Direction3; counts: Record<Direction3, number> }[];
   316	  trendConfound: { description: string; pairedAlwaysRisingAccuracy: number | null; naiveBeforeAfterAlwaysRisingAccuracy: number | null; sham: { n: number; naiveUp: number; pairedNone: number }; lowDose: { n: number; naiveUp: number; pairedNone: number } };
   317	  validNonNullYieldAndCost: { totalCellSteps: number; costPerUseful: number | null };
   318	  crossSeedEffectStability: { n: number; agree: number; rate: number | null };
   319	  notes: string[];
   320	}
   321	
   322	export function compare(p: Protocol, gen: Generated, envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[], lines: readonly PredictionLine[], proposers: readonly Proposer[]): Comparison {
   323	  const rows = caseRows(p, gen.results);
   324	  const minEffect = p.minEffect!;
   325	  const dev = tableFor(p, "dev", proposers, lines, envelopes, rows, (r) => r.split === "dev");
   326	  const noOracle = proposers.filter((x) => x.role !== "oracle");
   327	  const test = tableFor(p, "test", noOracle, lines, envelopes, rows, (r) => r.split === "test");
   328	  const limited = new Set(rows.filter((r) => measurementLimited(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect)).map((r) => r.caseId));
   329	  const testNL = tableFor(p, "test, measurement-limited cases excluded", noOracle, lines, envelopes, rows, (r) => r.split === "test" && !limited.has(r.caseId));
   330	  const strongest = [...dev.rows].filter((r) => (BASELINE_IDS as readonly string[]).includes(r.id)).sort((a, b) => (a.brierImputed ?? 9) - (b.brierImputed ?? 9) || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : 1))[0].id;
   331	
   332	  // Per-family Brier (imputed) per proposer, intervals only with enough families.
   333	  const perFamily: Comparison["perFamily"] = {};
   334	  for (const split of ["dev", "test"] as const) {
   335	    const sel = rows.filter((r) => r.split === split);
   336	    const envs = envelopes.filter((e) => sel.some((r) => r.caseId === e.caseId));
   337	    const fam: Record<string, Map<string, number>> = {};
   338	    for (const pr of noOracle) {
   339	      const sc = scoredCases(lines, pr.id, envs, sel, minEffect);
   340	      fam[pr.id] = familyMeans(sc.map((c) => ({ familyId: c.familyId, v: c.status === "answered" ? brierOf(c.prediction!.probs!, c.outcome) : brierOf(p.devPrior!, c.outcome) })));
   341	    }
   342	    const enough = new Set(sel.map((r) => r.familyId)).size >= MIN_FAMILIES_FOR_INTERVALS;
   343	    perFamily[split] = {
   344	      intervals: enough,
   345	      brierImputed: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(fam).map(([id, m]) => [id, familyBootstrap(m, p.bootstrap.resamples, p.bootstrap.seed)])),
   346	      pairedVsStrongest: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(fam).filter(([id]) => id !== strongest).map(([id, m]) => [id, familyBootstrap(pairedFamilyDiff(m, fam[strongest]), p.bootstrap.resamples, p.bootstrap.seed)])),
   347	    };
   348	  }
   349	
   350	  const mechanismAudit = p.mechanisms.map((m) => {
   351	    const counts: Record<Direction3, number> = { up: 0, down: 0, none: 0 };
   352	    for (const r of rows.filter((x) => x.mechanismId === m.id)) counts[labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect)]++;
   353	    return { mechanism: m.id, expected: m.expected, counts };
   354	  });
   355	
   356	  // Trend confound diagnostic: a naive predictor that says "up" whenever treated ends above its before-value, scored by the naive before/after rule vs the paired rule.
   357	  const acc = (f: (r: CaseRow) => Direction3): number | null => (rows.length ? rows.filter((r) => f(r) === "up").length / rows.length : null);
   358	  const naive = (r: CaseRow): Direction3 => labelOf(r.treated - r.before, minEffect);
   359	  const paired = (r: CaseRow): Direction3 => labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect);
   360	  const tally = (id: string) => {
   361	    const rs = rows.filter((r) => r.mechanismId === id);
   362	    return { n: rs.length, naiveUp: rs.filter((r) => naive(r) === "up").length, pairedNone: rs.filter((r) => paired(r) === "none").length };
   363	  };
   364	  const totalCellSteps = gen.results.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cellSteps, 0) + gen.variants.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cellSteps, 0);
   365	  const vr = caseRows(p, gen.variants);
   366	  const stab = signAgreement(
   367	    vr.map((v) => labelOf(rows.find((r) => r.caseId === v.caseId)!.effect.bodyMass, minEffect)),
   368	    vr.map((v) => labelOf(v.effect.bodyMass, minEffect)),
   369	  );
   370	  return {
   371	    protocolHash: protocolHash(p),
   372	    noModelStatement: "No model call, no GPU, no paid compute: every prediction is from a deterministic rule. This table says nothing about any model.",
   373	    strongestBaselineOnDev: strongest,
   374	    dev,
   375	    test,
   376	    testExcludingMeasurementLimited: testNL,
   377	    measurementLimitedCases: limited.size,
   378	    perFamily,
   379	    mechanismAudit,
   380	    trendConfound: {
   381	      description: "alwaysRising predicts 'up' for every case. Paired rule (treated minus control) vs naive before/after rule (treated minus value at the fork).",
   382	      pairedAlwaysRisingAccuracy: acc(paired),
   383	      naiveBeforeAfterAlwaysRisingAccuracy: acc(naive),
   384	      sham: tally("sham"),
   385	      lowDose: tally("lowDoseFeed"),
   386	    },
   387	    validNonNullYieldAndCost: { totalCellSteps, costPerUseful: costPerUseful(totalCellSteps, rows.map((r) => labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect))) },
   388	    crossSeedEffectStability: stab,
   389	    notes: [
   390	      `below ${MIN_FAMILIES_FOR_INTERVALS} families a split is descriptive only (no intervals)`,
   391	      "the strongest simple baseline is whichever of priorMajority, alwaysNone, fixedRule, numericTrend, v0Rules scores best on dev; a calibrated majority is legitimately strong",
   392	      "shuffledEvidence is a control, mechanismRule a dev-only oracle, abstainAll a coverage floor",
   393	    ],
   394	  };
   395	}
   396	
   397	export function checkLeaks(p: Protocol, envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[]): string[] {
   398	  return assertNoLeak(splitPlanOf(p), p.donors, envelopes);
   399	}
