   401	export const RUN_VERSIONS = { protocol: PROTOCOL_VERSION, envelope: ENVELOPE_VERSION, readouts: READOUTS_VERSION, ruleVersion: RULE_VERSION };
   402	export { caseIdOf, recipeHash, recipeOf };
     1	// Builds the canonical output files (PLAN.md, section 9) as strings, a pure function of the protocol and the generated results, so
     2	// two runs are byte-identical. Non-canonical metadata (git SHA, timing, hardware) is the CLI's, in meta.json.
     3	import { canonical, sha256Text } from "../propose.ts";
     4	import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "./envelope.ts";
     5	import { labelOf, recipeHash, recipeOf } from "./fixtures.ts";
     6	import { renderReport } from "./report-html.ts";
     7	import { BASELINE_IDS, buildProposers, caseRows, checkLeaks, compare, measurementLimited, protocolHash, runProposers, type Comparison, type Generated, type Protocol, type SplitTable } from "./run.ts";
     8	
     9	export const CANONICAL_FILES = ["envelopes.jsonl", "protocol.snapshot.json", "recipes.jsonl", "predictions.jsonl", "outcomes.jsonl", "comparison.json", "comparison.md", "report.html"] as const;
    10	
    11	const f3 = (v: number | null): string => (v === null ? "n/a" : v.toFixed(3));
    12	
    13	function tableMd(t: SplitTable): string {
    14	  const lines = [`### ${t.split} (${t.nCases} cases, ${t.nFamilies} families; up ${t.classCounts.up}, down ${t.classCounts.down}, none ${t.classCounts.none})`, "", "| proposer | role | answered | abstained | invalid | missing | coverage | Brier (imputed) | Brier (answered) | direction acc. | balanced acc. | acc. at matched coverage | valid non-null yield |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"];
    15	  for (const r of t.rows) lines.push(`| ${r.id} | ${r.role} | ${r.answered} | ${r.abstained} | ${r.invalid} | ${r.missing} | ${f3(r.coverage)} | ${f3(r.brierImputed)} | ${f3(r.brierAnswered)} | ${f3(r.directionalAccuracy)} | ${f3(r.balancedAccuracy)} | ${f3(r.accuracyAtMatchedCoverage)} | ${f3(r.validNonNullYield)} |`);
    16	  lines.push("", `Mean absolute effect: ${Object.entries(t.meanAbsEffect).map(([k, v]) => `${k} ${v === null ? "n/a" : v.toFixed(1)}`).join("; ")}.`, "");
    17	  return lines.join("\n");
    18	}
    19	
    20	export function comparisonMarkdown(c: Comparison, minEffect: number): string {
    21	  const out: string[] = ["# Prediction envelope v0: comparison", "", c.noModelStatement, "", `Protocol hash \`${c.protocolHash}\`. Primary readout: body mass (B+P); minimum effect ${minEffect} mass quanta. Strongest simple baseline on dev: **${c.strongestBaselineOnDev}**. Measurement-limited cases (label flips at 0.75x or 1.25x the threshold): ${c.measurementLimitedCases}, kept in every denominator of the first two tables.`, "", tableMd(c.dev), tableMd(c.test), tableMd(c.testExcludingMeasurementLimited)];
    22	  out.push("## Family-level intervals (imputed Brier, whole-family bootstrap)", "");
    23	  for (const [split, v] of Object.entries(c.perFamily)) {
    24	    out.push(`- ${split}: ${v.intervals ? "intervals computed" : "descriptive only (fewer than 10 families)"}`);
    25	    if (v.intervals) {
    26	      for (const [id, iv] of Object.entries(v.brierImputed)) if (iv) out.push(`  - ${id}: ${f3(iv.mean)} [${f3(iv.lo)}, ${f3(iv.hi)}]`);
    27	      for (const [id, iv] of Object.entries(v.pairedVsStrongest)) if (iv) out.push(`  - ${id} minus ${c.strongestBaselineOnDev}: ${f3(iv.mean)} [${f3(iv.lo)}, ${f3(iv.hi)}]`);
    28	    }
    29	  }
    30	  out.push("", "## Mechanism audit (measured vs fixture expectation)", "", "| mechanism | expected | up | down | none |", "|---|---|---|---|---|");
    31	  for (const m of c.mechanismAudit) out.push(`| ${m.mechanism} | ${m.expected} | ${m.counts.up} | ${m.counts.down} | ${m.counts.none} |`);
    32	  const t = c.trendConfound;
    33	  out.push("", "## Trend-confound diagnostic", "", t.description, "", `- share of cases labelled up: paired rule ${f3(t.pairedAlwaysRisingAccuracy)}, naive before/after rule ${f3(t.naiveBeforeAfterAlwaysRisingAccuracy)}`, `- sham: ${t.sham.n} cases, naive rule says up on ${t.sham.naiveUp}, paired rule says none on ${t.sham.pairedNone}`, `- low-dose feed: ${t.lowDose.n} cases, naive rule says up on ${t.lowDose.naiveUp}, paired rule says none on ${t.lowDose.pairedNone}`);
    34	  out.push("", "## Cost and stability", "", `- total evaluation cost ${c.validNonNullYieldAndCost.totalCellSteps} cell-steps; cost per non-null case ${c.validNonNullYieldAndCost.costPerUseful === null ? "n/a" : c.validNonNullYieldAndCost.costPerUseful.toFixed(0)}`, `- cross-seed effect stability (qualify families, sibling seed): ${c.crossSeedEffectStability.agree}/${c.crossSeedEffectStability.n} labels agree (${f3(c.crossSeedEffectStability.rate)}); heterogeneity across seeds of one family, not a predictor's accuracy limit`, "", ...c.notes.map((n) => `- ${n}`), "");
    35	  return out.join("\n");
    36	}
    37	
    38	const jl = (xs: readonly unknown[]): string => xs.map((x) => canonical(x) + "\n").join("");
    39	
    40	export interface Built {
    41	  files: Record<string, string>;
    42	  canonicalSha256: string;
    43	  comparison: Comparison;
    44	  leaks: string[];
    45	}
    46	
    47	export function buildOutputs(p: Protocol, gen: Generated): Built {
    48	  if (!p.frozen && !p.devPrior) throw new Error("protocol is not frozen");
    49	  const envelopes: PredictionEnvelope[] = gen.results.flatMap((r) => r.envelopes);
    50	  const leaks = checkLeaks(p, envelopes);
    51	  if (leaks.length) throw new Error(`leak check failed: ${leaks.join("; ")}`);
    52	  const proposers = buildProposers(p, envelopes);
    53	  const lines = runProposers(proposers, envelopes);
    54	  const comparison = compare(p, gen, envelopes, lines, proposers);
    55	  const rows = caseRows(p, gen.results);
    56	  const minEffect = p.minEffect!;
    57	  const pHash = protocolHash(p);
    58	  const files: Record<string, string> = {
    59	    "envelopes.jsonl": jl(envelopes),
    60	    "protocol.snapshot.json": canonical(p) + "\n",
    61	    "recipes.jsonl": jl([...gen.results, ...gen.variants].map((r) => {
    62	      const fam = p.families.find((f) => f.id === r.familyId)!;
    63	      const rec = recipeOf(p, fam, r.seed);
    64	      return { familyId: r.familyId, historyId: r.historyId, seed: r.seed, recipeHash: recipeHash(rec), controlHash: r.controlHash, neutralityChecks: r.neutralityChecks, recipe: rec };
    65	    })),
    66	    "predictions.jsonl": jl(lines),
    67	    "outcomes.jsonl": jl(rows.map((r) => ({ ...r, label: labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect), measurementLimited: measurementLimited(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect), protocolHash: pHash }))),
    68	    "comparison.json": canonical(comparison) + "\n",
    69	    "comparison.md": comparisonMarkdown(comparison, minEffect),
    70	  };
    71	  files["report.html"] = renderReport({ comparison, envelopes, lines, rows, minEffect, proposerIds: proposers.map((x) => x.id) });
    72	  const canonicalSha256 = CANONICAL_FILES.map((n) => `${sha256Text(files[n])}  ${n}\n`).join("");
    73	  files["canonical.sha256"] = canonicalSha256;
    74	  return { files, canonicalSha256, comparison, leaks };
    75	}

     1	// Static notebook view (PLAN.md, section 10): per case an observation block (census sparkline, frame thumbnails), a hypothesis
     2	// block (probabilities per proposer, with the free-text reason, escaped) and a measured-outcome block (the paired result), visibly
     3	// distinct. Failed predictions first; invalid or missing ones listed in their own section. No scripts, no external URLs, works from
     4	// file://. A pure function of its inputs.
     5	import { escapeHtml } from "../report-html/page.ts";
     6	import { labelOf } from "./fixtures.ts";
     7	import type { PredictionEnvelope } from "./envelope.ts";
     8	import type { CaseRow, Comparison, PredictionLine, SplitTable } from "./run.ts";
     9	import { argmax, validatePredictionFile } from "./validate.ts";
    10	
    11	const esc = escapeHtml;
    12	const pct = (v: number | null): string => (v === null ? "n/a" : v.toFixed(3));
    13	
    14	function spark(env: PredictionEnvelope): string {
    15	  const pts = env.census.map((c) => c.mass);
    16	  const lo = Math.min(...pts), hi = Math.max(...pts), span = Math.max(1, hi - lo);
    17	  const w = 160, h = 40;
    18	  const d = pts.map((v, i) => `${((i / Math.max(1, pts.length - 1)) * w).toFixed(1)},${(h - 2 - ((v - lo) / span) * (h - 4)).toFixed(1)}`).join(" ");
    19	  return `<svg class="spark" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" role="img" aria-label="body mass over the observed steps ${env.census[0].step} to ${env.census[env.census.length - 1].step}"><polyline points="${d}" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5"/></svg>`;
    20	}
    21	/** The last frame as four-level quantised paths (one path per level, empty blocks omitted), to keep the page small. */
    22	function thumb(f: PredictionEnvelope["frames"][number]): string {
    23	  const max = Math.max(1, ...f.mass), s = 6;
    24	  const paths: string[] = ["", "", "", ""];
    25	  f.mass.forEach((m, i) => {
    26	    if (m <= 0) return;
    27	    const lvl = Math.min(3, Math.floor((m / max) * 4));
    28	    paths[lvl] += `M${(i % f.side) * s} ${Math.floor(i / f.side) * s}h${s}v${s}h-${s}z`;
    29	  });
    30	  const body = paths.map((d, i) => (d ? `<path d="${d}" fill="currentColor" fill-opacity="${[0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1][i]}"/>` : "")).join("");
    31	  return `<figure class="thumb"><svg viewBox="0 0 ${f.side * s} ${f.side * s}" role="img" aria-label="mass by block at step ${f.step}">${body}</svg><figcaption>t=${f.step}</figcaption></figure>`;
    32	}
    33	function bar(p: { up: number; down: number; none: number }): string {
    34	  return `<span class="bar" aria-hidden="true"><i class="up" style="width:${p.up / 10}%"></i><i class="down" style="width:${p.down / 10}%"></i><i class="none" style="width:${p.none / 10}%"></i></span>`;
    35	}
    36	
    37	function tableHtml(t: SplitTable): string {
    38	  const head = ["proposer", "role", "n", "answered", "abstained", "invalid", "missing", "coverage", "Brier (imputed)", "Brier (answered)", "direction acc.", "balanced acc.", "acc. @ matched coverage", "valid non-null yield"];
    39	  const body = t.rows.map((r) => `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(r.id)}</th><td>${esc(r.role)}</td><td>${r.n}</td><td>${r.answered}</td><td>${r.abstained}</td><td>${r.invalid}</td><td>${r.missing}</td><td>${pct(r.coverage)}</td><td>${pct(r.brierImputed)}</td><td>${pct(r.brierAnswered)}</td><td>${pct(r.directionalAccuracy)}</td><td>${pct(r.balancedAccuracy)}</td><td>${pct(r.accuracyAtMatchedCoverage)}</td><td>${pct(r.validNonNullYield)}</td></tr>`).join("");
    40	  return `<h3>${esc(t.split)} (${t.nCases} cases, ${t.nFamilies} families; up ${t.classCounts.up}, down ${t.classCounts.down}, none ${t.classCounts.none})</h3><div class="scroll"><table><thead><tr>${head.map((h) => `<th scope="col">${esc(h)}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${body}</tbody></table></div>`;
    41	}
    42	
    43	export interface ReportInput {
    44	  comparison: Comparison;
    45	  envelopes: readonly PredictionEnvelope[];
    46	  lines: readonly PredictionLine[];
    47	  rows: readonly CaseRow[];
    48	  minEffect: number;
    49	  proposerIds: readonly string[];
    50	}
    51	
    52	export function renderReport(inp: ReportInput): string {
    53	  const { comparison: c, envelopes, lines, rows, minEffect, proposerIds } = inp;
    54	  const rowOf = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.caseId, r]));
    55	  // Per-proposer validation (so invalid lines show up here as they do in the scorer).
    56	  const status = new Map<string, Map<string, "answered" | "abstained" | "invalid" | "missing">>();
    57	  const preds = new Map<string, Map<string, PredictionLine["prediction"] | null>>();
    58	  for (const id of proposerIds) {
    59	    const chk = validatePredictionFile(lines.filter((l) => l.proposer === id).map((l) => JSON.stringify(l.prediction)), envelopes);
    60	    const s = new Map<string, "answered" | "abstained" | "invalid" | "missing">(), pm = new Map<string, PredictionLine["prediction"] | null>();
    61	    for (const e of envelopes) {
    62	      const g = chk.byCase.get(e.caseId);
    63	      s.set(e.caseId, !g ? "missing" : !g.prediction ? "invalid" : g.prediction.abstain ? "abstained" : "answered");
    64	      pm.set(e.caseId, g?.prediction ?? null);
    65	    }
    66	    status.set(id, s);
    67	    preds.set(id, pm);
    68	  }
    69	  const failures = (caseId: string): number => {
    70	    const r = rowOf.get(caseId)!;
    71	    const o = labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect);
    72	    let k = 0;
    73	    for (const id of proposerIds) {
    74	      const st = status.get(id)!.get(caseId)!;
    75	      const p = preds.get(id)!.get(caseId);
    76	      if (st === "invalid" || st === "missing" || (st === "answered" && p?.probs && argmax(p.probs) !== o)) k++;
    77	    }
    78	    return k;
    79	  };
    80	  const ordered = [...envelopes].filter((e) => rowOf.has(e.caseId)).sort((a, b) => failures(b.caseId) - failures(a.caseId) || (a.caseId < b.caseId ? -1 : 1));
    81	  const caseHtml = (e: PredictionEnvelope): string => {
    82	    const r = rowOf.get(e.caseId)!;
    83	    const o = labelOf(r.effect.bodyMass, minEffect);
    84	    const hyp = proposerIds
    85	      .map((id) => {
    86	        const st = status.get(id)!.get(e.caseId)!;
    87	        const p = preds.get(id)!.get(e.caseId);
    88	        if (!p || st === "invalid" || st === "missing") return `<li class="bad"><b>${esc(id)}</b> ${esc(st)}</li>`;
    89	        if (p.abstain) return `<li><b>${esc(id)}</b> abstains <q>${esc(p.reason ?? "")}</q></li>`;
    90	        const a = argmax(p.probs!);
    91	        return `<li class="${a === o ? "ok" : "bad"}"><b>${esc(id)}</b> ${bar(p.probs!)} up ${p.probs!.up} / down ${p.probs!.down} / none ${p.probs!.none} per-mille, says ${esc(a)} <q>${esc(p.reason ?? "")}</q></li>`;
    92	      })
    93	      .join("");
    94	    const op = e.task.op;
    95	    const opText = op.kind === "none" ? "no operation (sham)" : `${op.kind} at (${op.x}, ${op.y}) radius ${op.r}${op.amount !== undefined ? `, amount ${op.amount}` : ""}`;
    96	    return `<article id="${esc(e.caseId)}" class="case"><h3>${esc(e.caseId)} <small>family ${esc(e.familyId)}, ${esc(r.split)}</small></h3>
    97	<section class="obs"><h4>Observation (steps up to ${e.task.forkStep})</h4>${spark(e)}<div class="thumbs">${thumb(e.frames[e.frames.length - 1])}</div></section>
    98	<section class="hyp"><h4>Hypotheses</h4><p>Question: ${esc(opText)} at step ${e.task.forkStep + e.task.delta}; direction of the ${esc(e.task.readout)} difference at step ${e.task.forkStep + e.task.horizon}.</p><ul>${hyp}</ul></section>
    99	<section class="out"><h4>Measured outcome</h4><p>treated ${r.treated}, control ${r.control}, effect ${r.effect.bodyMass} (minimum effect ${minEffect}) so <b>${esc(o)}</b>; fixture expectation ${esc(r.expected)}${o === r.expected ? "" : " (measured differs)"}.</p></section></article>`;
   100	  };
   101	  const bad = ordered.filter((e) => proposerIds.some((id) => ["invalid", "missing"].includes(status.get(id)!.get(e.caseId)!)));
   102	  const css = `body{font:15px/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif;max-width:72rem;margin:0 auto;padding:1rem;color:#1d2328;background:#fbfaf6}table{border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px}th,td{border:1px solid #cfc9bb;padding:.25rem .5rem;text-align:left}.scroll{overflow-x:auto}.case{border:1px solid #cfc9bb;border-radius:6px;margin:1rem 0;padding:.5rem 1rem;display:grid;gap:.5rem}.obs{background:#eef3f6;padding:.5rem;border-radius:4px;color:#27475a}.hyp{background:#fbf3e3;padding:.5rem;border-radius:4px}.out{background:#e8f2e8;padding:.5rem;border-radius:4px}.spark{width:10rem;height:2.5rem}.thumbs{display:flex;gap:.5rem;flex-wrap:wrap}.thumb{margin:0}.thumb svg{width:3.5rem;height:3.5rem;background:#fff;border:1px solid #cfc9bb}.thumb figcaption{font-size:11px}ul{padding-left:1rem;margin:.25rem 0}li.ok b{color:#1f6b2a}li.bad b{color:#9b2c1c}q{color:#555}.bar{display:inline-flex;width:6rem;height:.6rem;vertical-align:middle;margin-right:.4rem;background:#ddd}.bar i{display:block;height:100%}.bar .up{background:#2a7a3a}.bar .down{background:#b23b2a}.bar .none{background:#8a8f94}small{color:#666;font-weight:normal}code{overflow-wrap:anywhere}`;
   103	  return `<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>Prediction envelope v0: notebook</title><style>${css}</style></head><body>
   104	<h1>Prediction envelope v0: notebook</h1>
   105	<p>${esc(c.noModelStatement)}</p>
   106	<p>Protocol hash <code>${esc(c.protocolHash)}</code>. Strongest simple baseline on dev: <b>${esc(c.strongestBaselineOnDev)}</b>. Primary readout: body mass (B+P), minimum effect ${minEffect}. Failed predictions first.</p>
   107	<h2>Comparison</h2>${tableHtml(c.dev)}${tableHtml(c.test)}${tableHtml(c.testExcludingMeasurementLimited)}
   108	<h2>Invalid or missing predictions</h2>${bad.length ? `<ul>${bad.map((e) => `<li><a href="#${esc(e.caseId)}">${esc(e.caseId)}</a></li>`).join("")}</ul>` : "<p>None.</p>"}
   109	<h2>Cases</h2>${ordered.map(caseHtml).join("\n")}
   110	</body></html>
   111	`;
   112	}
# Prediction envelope v0: comparison

No model call, no GPU, no paid compute: every prediction is from a deterministic rule. This table says nothing about any model.

Protocol hash `caa4b865566c894fbc7373dee7ec4c59033c71961b134839e7edcc4c1d46e467`. Primary readout: body mass (B+P); minimum effect 50 mass quanta. Strongest simple baseline on dev: **v0Rules**. Measurement-limited cases (label flips at 0.75x or 1.25x the threshold): 13, kept in every denominator of the first two tables.

## Mechanism audit (measured vs fixture expectation)

| mechanism | expected | up | down | none |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| feedOccupied | up | 22 | 0 | 2 |
| lesionOccupied | down | 0 | 24 | 0 |
| drainOccupied | down | 0 | 8 | 16 |
| sham | none | 0 | 0 | 24 |
| lesionEmpty | none | 0 | 0 | 24 |
| lowDoseFeed | none | 0 | 0 | 24 |

## Trend-confound diagnostic

alwaysRising predicts 'up' for every case. Paired rule (treated minus control) vs naive before/after rule (treated minus value at the fork).

- share of cases labelled up: paired rule 0.153, naive before/after rule 0.000
- sham: 24 cases, naive rule says up on 0, paired rule says none on 24
- low-dose feed: 24 cases, naive rule says up on 0, paired rule says none on 24

## Cost and stability

- total evaluation cost 57344000 cell-steps; cost per non-null case 1061926
- cross-seed effect stability (qualify families, sibling seed): 21/24 labels agree (0.875); heterogeneity across seeds of one family, not a predictor's accuracy limit

- below 10 families a split is descriptive only (no intervals)
- the strongest simple baseline is whichever of priorMajority, alwaysNone, fixedRule, numericTrend, v0Rules scores best on dev; a calibrated majority is legitimately strong
- shuffledEvidence is a control, mechanismRule a dev-only oracle, abstainAll a coverage floor

     1	// Shared builders for the prediction-envelope tests: synthetic envelopes with no simulation, and a tiny protocol for the CPU fixtures.
     2	import { defaultConfig } from "@bl/schema";
     3	import { sealEnvelope, standardMenu, type CensusPoint, type Frame, type PredictionEnvelope, type TaskOp } from "../lib/prediction/envelope.ts";
     4	import { draftProtocol, type Protocol } from "../lib/prediction/run.ts";
     5	
     6	export function censusSeries(start: number, slope: number, n = 31, every = 10): CensusPoint[] {
     7	  return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ step: i * every, livingCells: 100, freeNutrient: 50, mass: start + slope * i * every }));
     8	}
     9	export function frames(): Frame[] {
    10	  const mass = new Array(64).fill(0);
    11	  mass[0] = 5;
    12	  return [{ step: 300, side: 8, mass }];
    13	}
    14	export function synthEnv(op: TaskOp, o: { familyId?: string; historyId?: string; caseId?: string; census?: CensusPoint[]; delta?: number; horizon?: number } = {}): PredictionEnvelope {
    15	  const cfg = defaultConfig({ tileW: 32, tileH: 32, kernelRadius: 4, seed: 1 });
    16	  return sealEnvelope({
    17	    version: 1,
    18	    caseId: o.caseId ?? "c-test",
    19	    historyId: o.historyId ?? `${o.familyId ?? "fA"}:s1`,
    20	    familyId: o.familyId ?? "fA",
    21	    ruleVersion: 1,
    22	    config: cfg,
    23	    census: o.census ?? censusSeries(1000, 0),
    24	    frames: frames(),
    25	    menu: standardMenu(cfg, 60, 4096, 6),
    26	    task: { subject: "world", op, delta: o.delta ?? 10, readout: "bodyMass", forkStep: 300, horizon: o.horizon ?? 200 },
    27	  });
    28	}
    29	export const LESION: TaskOp = { kind: "lesion", x: 1, y: 1, r: 4 };
    30	export const FEED: TaskOp = { kind: "feed", x: 1, y: 1, r: 4, amount: 64 };
    31	export const DRAIN: TaskOp = { kind: "drain", x: 1, y: 1, r: 4, amount: 64 };
    32	export const SHAM: TaskOp = { kind: "none" };
    33	
    34	/** A tiny protocol: 6 short families, 2 mechanisms' worth of work per family, short windows. minEffect and devPrior are set. */
    35	export function tinyProtocol(): Protocol {
    36	  const p = draftProtocol();
    37	  p.families = p.families.slice(0, 6);
    38	  p.nDev = 3;
    39	  p.nQualify = 2;
    40	  p.window = { forkStep: 40, delta: 5, horizon: 30, censusEvery: 10, frameSteps: [20, 40] };
    41	  p.mechanisms = p.mechanisms.filter((m) => ["feedOccupied", "lesionOccupied", "sham"].includes(m.id));
    42	  p.proposers.numericTrend.window = 2;
    43	  p.minEffect = 10;
    44	  p.devPrior = { up: 200, down: 300, none: 500 };
    45	  p.frozen = true;
    46	  p.donors = {};
    47	  return p;
    48	}
    49	
    50	import { buildOutputs, type Built } from "../lib/prediction/output.ts";
    51	import { generate, splitPlanOf } from "../lib/prediction/run.ts";
    52	import { donorsFor } from "../lib/prediction/splits.ts";
    53	
    54	let cached: { p: Protocol; built: Built } | undefined;
    55	/** A complete tiny pipeline (4 families, 3 mechanisms) built once per test file. */
    56	export function tinyPipeline(): { p: Protocol; built: Built } {
    57	  if (cached) return cached;
    58	  const p = tinyProtocol();
    59	  p.families = p.families.slice(0, 4);
    60	  p.nDev = 2;
    61	  p.nQualify = 1;
    62	  p.donors = donorsFor(splitPlanOf(p), p.salt);
    63	  const gen = generate(p, p.families.map((f) => f.id), true);
    64	  cached = { p, built: buildOutputs(p, gen) };
    65	  return cached;
    66	}

    11	describe("validatePrediction", () => {
    12	  it("accepts menu lesion, feed, drain and sham tasks", () => {
    13	    for (const op of [LESION, FEED, DRAIN, SHAM]) {
    14	      const env = synthEnv(op);
    15	      expect(validatePrediction(good(env), env)).toEqual({ ok: true, errors: [] });

    17	  });
    18	  it("accepts an abstention without probs", () => {
    19	    const env = synthEnv(LESION);
    20	    const { probs: _p, ...rest } = good(env);
    21	    void _p;
    22	    expect(validatePrediction({ ...rest, abstain: true }, env).ok).toBe(true);
    23	  });
    24	  it.each([
    25	    ["sum 999", { up: 100, down: 700, none: 199 }],
    26	    ["sum 1001", { up: 100, down: 700, none: 201 }],
    27	    ["float", { up: 100.5, down: 699.5, none: 200 }],
    28	    ["negative", { up: -100, down: 900, none: 200 }],
    29	    ["over 1000", { up: 0, down: 1001, none: -1 }],
    30	    ["extra class", { up: 0, down: 500, none: 500, maybe: 0 }],
    31	    ["missing class", { up: 500, down: 500 }],
    32	  ])("rejects probs with %s", (_n, probs) => {
    33	    const env = synthEnv(LESION);
    34	    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), probs }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    35	  });
    36	  it("rejects an abstention that carries probs, and missing probs without abstaining", () => {
    37	    const env = synthEnv(LESION);
    38	    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), abstain: true }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    39	    const { probs: _p, ...rest } = good(env);
    40	    void _p;
    41	    expect(validatePrediction({ ...rest, abstain: false }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    42	  });
    43	  it("rejects a task that differs in readout, intervention or time (the question is fixed)", () => {
    44	    const env = synthEnv(LESION);
    45	    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), task: { ...env.task, readout: "livingCells" } }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    46	    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), task: { ...env.task, op: { kind: "none" } } }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    47	    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), task: { ...env.task, delta: 11 } }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    48	    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), task: { ...env.task, horizon: 100 } }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    49	  });
    50	  it("rejects extra keys, wrong caseId or hash, __proto__ keys and non-objects", () => {
    51	    const env = synthEnv(LESION);
    52	    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), extra: 1 }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    53	    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), caseId: "other" }, env).ok).toBe(false);
    54	    expect(validatePrediction({ ...good(env), envelopeHash: "0".repeat(64) }, env).ok).toBe(false);

    56	    expect(validatePrediction(JSON.parse(`{"constructor":1}`), env).ok).toBe(false);

    58	  });
    59	});

    14	describe("proposers", () => {
    15	  it("every baseline emits validator-clean output on every envelope", () => {
    16	    const ps = [fixedRule(K.fixedRule), numericTrend(K.numericTrend), priorMajority({ up: 100, down: 200, none: 700 }), alwaysNone(), abstainAll(), v0Rules(K.v0Rules), mechanismRule(K.mechanismRule)];
    17	    for (const p of ps) for (const e of envs) expect(validatePrediction(p.propose(e), e), `${p.id} on ${e.caseId}`).toEqual({ ok: true, errors: [] });
    18	  });
    19	  it("fixedRule follows the operation and ignores the census", () => {
    20	    const p = fixedRule(K.fixedRule);
    21	    const a = synthEnv(FEED, { census: censusSeries(1000, 5) }), b = synthEnv(FEED, { census: censusSeries(9, -3) });
    22	    expect(p.propose(a).probs).toEqual(p.propose(b).probs);
    23	    expect(p.propose(a).probs).toEqual({ up: 600, down: 200, none: 200 });
    24	    expect(p.propose(synthEnv(LESION)).probs!.down).toBe(600);
    25	    expect(p.propose(synthEnv(DRAIN)).probs!.down).toBe(600);
    26	    expect(p.propose(synthEnv(SHAM)).probs!.none).toBe(600);
    27	  });
    28	  it("numericTrend ignores frames and the task", () => {
    29	    const p = numericTrend(K.numericTrend);
    30	    const a = synthEnv(FEED, { census: censusSeries(1000, 2) });
    31	    const b = { ...synthEnv(LESION, { census: censusSeries(1000, 2) }), frames: [] };
    32	    expect(p.propose(a).probs).toEqual(p.propose(b as never).probs);
    33	    expect(trendDirection(censusSeries(1000, 2), 200, K.numericTrend)).toBe("up");
    34	    expect(trendDirection(censusSeries(1000, -2), 200, K.numericTrend)).toBe("down");
    35	    expect(trendDirection(censusSeries(1000, 0), 200, K.numericTrend)).toBe("none");
    36	  });
    37	  it("alwaysNone and priorMajority are valid forecasts; abstainAll abstains", () => {
    38	    const e = envs[0];
    39	    expect(alwaysNone().propose(e).probs).toEqual({ up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 });
    40	    expect(priorMajority({ up: 139, down: 208, none: 653 }).propose(e).probs).toEqual({ up: 139, down: 208, none: 653 });
    41	    expect(abstainAll().propose(e).abstain).toBe(true);
    42	  });
    43	  it("leadProbs and toPerMille always sum to 1000", () => {
    44	    for (const lead of [334, 500, 601, 999]) for (const d of ["up", "down", "none"] as const) {
    45	      const p = leadProbs(d, lead);
    46	      expect(p.up + p.down + p.none).toBe(1000);
    47	      expect(p[d]).toBe(lead);
    48	    }
    49	    const q = toPerMille({ up: 1, down: 1, none: 1 });
    50	    expect(q.up + q.down + q.none).toBe(1000);
    51	  });
    52	  it("the v0 rule proposer maps through fromRulesProposal (direction mass, remainder on none)", () => {
    53	    const e = synthEnv(FEED);
    54	    expect(v0Rules(K.v0Rules).propose(e).probs).toEqual({ up: 600, down: 0, none: 400 });
    55	    expect(v0Rules(K.v0Rules).propose(synthEnv(SHAM)).probs).toEqual({ up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 });
    56	    expect(fromRulesProposal(e, { kind: "directional", statement: "s", direction: RULES.livingCells.high.direction }, 600).probs).toEqual({ up: 0, down: 600, none: 400 });
    57	  });
    58	  it("hashes of existing v0 helpers are unchanged by the additive export", () => {
    59	    expect(canonical({ b: 1, a: [2, { d: 1, c: 2 }] })).toBe('{"a":[2,{"c":2,"d":1}],"b":1}');
    60	  });
    61	});
    62	
    63	describe("shuffledEvidence", () => {
    64	  const plan = assignSplits(["fA", "fB", "fC", "fD", "fE", "fF"], "salt", 3, 1);
    65	  const donors = donorsFor(plan, "salt");
    66	  const byFam = new Map(plan.dev.concat(plan.test).map((f, i) => [f, synthEnv(FEED, { familyId: f, caseId: `d-${f}`, census: censusSeries(1000, i - 2) })]));
    67	  const splitOf = (f: string) => (plan.dev.includes(f) ? "dev" : plan.test.includes(f) ? "test" : undefined) as "dev" | "test" | undefined;
    68	  it("is deterministic, uses a different family of the same split, and equals numericTrend on the donor's census", () => {
    69	    const p = shuffledEvidence(K.numericTrend, donors, (f) => byFam.get(f), splitOf);
    70	    for (const [f, e] of byFam) {
    71	      const d = donors[f];
    72	      expect(d).not.toBe(f);
    73	      expect(splitOf(d)).toBe(splitOf(f));
    74	      expect(p.propose(e)).toEqual(p.propose(e));
    75	      expect(p.propose(e).probs).toEqual(numericTrend(K.numericTrend).propose(byFam.get(d)!).probs);
    76	    }
    77	  });
    78	  it("refuses a later window of the same history, and a donor from another split", () => {
    79	    const own = synthEnv(FEED, { familyId: "fA", historyId: "fA:s1", caseId: "own" });
    80	    const later = synthEnv(FEED, { familyId: "fZ", historyId: "fA:s1", caseId: "later" });
    81	    const p = shuffledEvidence(K.numericTrend, { fA: "fZ" }, () => later, () => "dev");
    82	    expect(() => p.propose(own)).toThrow(/same history/);
    83	    const cross = shuffledEvidence(K.numericTrend, { fA: "fB" }, () => synthEnv(FEED, { familyId: "fB", caseId: "x" }), (f) => (f === "fA" ? "dev" : "test"));
    84	    expect(() => cross.propose(own)).toThrow(/another split/);
    85	    const same = shuffledEvidence(K.numericTrend, { fA: "fA" }, () => own, () => "dev");
    86	    expect(() => same.propose(own)).toThrow(/same family/);
    87	  });
    88	});

    90	describe("module hygiene", () => {
    91	  it("the model seam and the proposers import nothing outside the repo and no network API", () => {
    92	    for (const f of ["model-seam.ts", "proposers.ts", "validate.ts", "score.ts"]) {
    93	      const src = readFileSync(new URL(`../lib/prediction/${f}`, import.meta.url), "utf8");
    94	      const mods = [...src.matchAll(/from\s+"([^"]+)"/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
    95	      for (const m of mods) expect(m.startsWith(".") || m.startsWith("@bl/"), `${f} imports ${m}`).toBe(true);
    96	      expect(src).not.toMatch(/\bfetch\s*\(|XMLHttpRequest|WebSocket|node:http|node:net/);
    97	    }
    98	  });
    99	});
     1	// Splits and leak checks (PLAN.md, section 7).
     2	import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     3	import { assertNoLeak, assignSplits, derangement, donorAllowed, donorsFor } from "../lib/prediction/splits.ts";
     4	import { FEED, censusSeries, synthEnv } from "./prediction-helpers.ts";
     5	
     6	const ids = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => `f${i}`);
     7	
     8	describe("splits", () => {
     9	  it("never lets a family straddle dev and test, and qualify lies inside dev", () => {
    10	    const p = assignSplits(ids, "salt", 6, 2);
    11	    expect(p.dev).toHaveLength(6);
    12	    expect(p.test).toHaveLength(6);
    13	    expect(p.dev.filter((f) => p.test.includes(f))).toEqual([]);
    14	    expect(p.qualify.every((f) => p.dev.includes(f))).toBe(true);
    15	    expect([...p.dev, ...p.test].sort()).toEqual([...ids].sort());
    16	  });
    17	  it("is deterministic under input permutation and depends on the salt", () => {
    18	    const a = assignSplits(ids, "salt", 6, 2);
    19	    expect(assignSplits([...ids].reverse(), "salt", 6, 2)).toEqual(a);
    20	    expect(assignSplits(ids, "other", 6, 2)).not.toEqual(a);
    21	  });
    22	  it("a derangement has no fixed point and stays inside its split", () => {
    23	    const p = assignSplits(ids, "salt", 6, 2);
    24	    const d = donorsFor(p, "salt");
    25	    for (const f of p.dev) expect(p.dev).toContain(d[f]);
    26	    for (const f of p.test) expect(p.test).toContain(d[f]);
    27	    for (const f of ids) expect(d[f]).not.toBe(f);
    28	    expect(new Set(Object.values(d)).size).toBe(ids.length);
    29	    expect(derangement(["a", "b"], "s")).toEqual({ a: "b", b: "a" });
    30	    expect(() => derangement(["a"], "s")).toThrow();
    31	  });
    32	  it("donorAllowed refuses the same family, the same history and another split", () => {
    33	    const a = { familyId: "fA", historyId: "fA:s1" };
    34	    const sp = (f: string) => (f === "fX" ? "test" : "dev") as "dev" | "test";
    35	    expect(donorAllowed(a, { familyId: "fA", historyId: "fA:s2" }, sp).ok).toBe(false);
    36	    expect(donorAllowed(a, { familyId: "fB", historyId: "fA:s1" }, sp).ok).toBe(false);
    37	    expect(donorAllowed(a, { familyId: "fX", historyId: "fX:s1" }, sp).ok).toBe(false);
    38	    expect(donorAllowed(a, { familyId: "fB", historyId: "fB:s1" }, sp).ok).toBe(true);
    39	  });
    40	});
    41	
    42	describe("assertNoLeak", () => {
    43	  const p = assignSplits(ids, "salt", 6, 2);
    44	  const d = donorsFor(p, "salt");
    45	  const envs = ids.map((f, i) => synthEnv(FEED, { familyId: f, caseId: `c${f}`, census: censusSeries(1000 + i, 1) }));
    46	  it("is clean on a sound plan", () => {
    47	    expect(assertNoLeak(p, d, envs)).toEqual([]);
    48	  });
    49	  it("fails on an injected shared family", () => {
    50	    expect(assertNoLeak({ ...p, test: [...p.test, p.dev[0]] }, d, envs).join()).toMatch(/both dev and test/);
    51	  });
    52	  it("fails on a bad donor (self or cross-split)", () => {
    53	    expect(assertNoLeak(p, { ...d, [p.dev[0]]: p.dev[0] }, envs).join()).toMatch(/own donor/);
    54	    expect(assertNoLeak(p, { ...d, [p.dev[0]]: p.test[0] }, envs).join()).toMatch(/another split/);
    55	  });
    56	  it("fails when a test envelope carries a dev census, or a history sits under two families", () => {
    57	    const dup = envs.map((e) => (e.familyId === p.test[0] ? { ...e, census: envs.find((x) => x.familyId === p.dev[0])!.census } : e));
    58	    expect(assertNoLeak(p, d, dup).join()).toMatch(/dev census/);
    59	    const hist = envs.map((e) => (e.familyId === p.test[0] ? { ...e, historyId: envs[0].historyId } : e));
    60	    expect(assertNoLeak(p, d, hist).join()).toMatch(/two families/);
    61	  });
    62	});

   118	describe("trend-confound truth table", () => {
   119	  // Hand-specified: a rising world (before = 100). Paired effect of sham and a low-dose feed is 0 (none); the naive before/after
   120	  // rule sees treated 160 - before 100 = +60 and calls both "up" at a minimum effect of 50.
   121	  const rows = [
   122	    { name: "sham", before: 100, treated: 160, control: 160, pairedLabel: "none", naiveLabel: "up" },
   123	    { name: "lowDose", before: 100, treated: 163, control: 160, pairedLabel: "none", naiveLabel: "up" },
   124	    { name: "feed", before: 100, treated: 260, control: 160, pairedLabel: "up", naiveLabel: "up" },

   126	  ] as const;
   127	  it("matches the hand-derived table", () => {
   128	    for (const r of rows) {
   129	      expect(labelOf(r.treated - r.control, 50), r.name).toBe(r.pairedLabel);
   130	      expect(labelOf(r.treated - r.before, 50), r.name).toBe(r.naiveLabel);
   131	    }
   132	    // alwaysRising ("up" everywhere): right on 2 of 4 by the paired rule, 3 of 4 by the naive rule.
   133	    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.pairedLabel === "up").length).toBe(1);
   134	    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.naiveLabel === "up").length).toBe(3);

     1	// Scoring, with hand-computed expectations (PLAN.md, section 8).
     2	import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     3	import type { Direction3 } from "../lib/prediction/envelope.ts";
     4	import { MIN_FAMILIES_FOR_INTERVALS, brierOf, costPerUseful, familyBootstrap, familyMeans, matchedCoverageSet, reliabilityTable, scoreProposer, signAgreement, type ScoredCase } from "../lib/prediction/score.ts";
     5	import type { Prediction, Probs } from "../lib/prediction/validate.ts";
     6	
     7	const pred = (probs: Probs): Prediction => ({ version: 1, caseId: "x", envelopeHash: "h", task: {} as never, probs, abstain: false });
     8	const mk = (i: number, outcome: Direction3, probs: Probs | null, status: ScoredCase["status"] = probs ? "answered" : "abstained", family = `f${i % 3}`): ScoredCase => ({ caseId: `c${i}`, familyId: family, outcome, prediction: probs ? pred(probs) : null, status });
     9	const PRIOR: Probs = { up: 200, down: 300, none: 500 };
    10	
    11	describe("multiclass Brier", () => {
    12	  it("uniform = 2/3, perfect = 0, confidently wrong = 2", () => {
    13	    expect(brierOf({ up: 333, down: 333, none: 334 }, "up")).toBeCloseTo(2 / 3, 2);
    14	    expect(brierOf({ up: 1000, down: 0, none: 0 }, "up")).toBe(0);
    15	    expect(brierOf({ up: 0, down: 1000, none: 0 }, "up")).toBe(2);
    16	  });
    17	  it("matches a hand-computed 6-case table", () => {
    18	    // per case: sum (p - o)^2
    19	    const table: [Direction3, Probs][] = [
    20	      ["up", { up: 600, down: 200, none: 200 }], // .16+.04+.04 = .24
    21	      ["down", { up: 100, down: 700, none: 200 }], // .01+.09+.04 = .14
    22	      ["none", { up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 }], // 0
    23	      ["none", { up: 500, down: 0, none: 500 }], // .25+0+.25 = .5
    24	      ["up", { up: 0, down: 500, none: 500 }], // 1+.25+.25 = 1.5
    25	      ["down", { up: 300, down: 300, none: 400 }], // .09+.49+.16 = .74
    26	    ];
    27	    const cs = table.map(([o, p], i) => mk(i, o, p));
    28	    const s = scoreProposer("t", cs, PRIOR);
    29	    expect(s.brierImputed).toBeCloseTo((0.24 + 0.14 + 0 + 0.5 + 1.5 + 0.74) / 6, 10);
    30	    expect(s.brierAnswered).toBeCloseTo(s.brierImputed!, 10);
    31	    // argmax with ties none > down > up: up (hit), down (hit), none (hit), none (tie none/up, hit), none (tie down/none, outcome up: miss), none (outcome down: miss)
    32	    expect(s.directionalAccuracy).toBeCloseTo(4 / 6, 10);
    33	  });
    34	  it("scores a majority-class forecast on an imbalanced set as computed", () => {
    35	    const cs = [...Array(9)].map((_, i) => mk(i, "none", { up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 })).concat([mk(9, "up", { up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 })]);
    36	    const s = scoreProposer("alwaysNone", cs, PRIOR);
    37	    expect(s.brierImputed).toBeCloseTo((9 * 0 + 2) / 10, 10);
    38	    expect(s.directionalAccuracy).toBe(0.9);
    39	    // balanced accuracy exposes it: recall(none) = 1, recall(up) = 0, two classes present.
    40	    expect(s.balancedAccuracy).toBe(0.5);
    41	    expect(s.balancedClasses).toBe(2);
    42	  });
    43	});
    44	
    45	describe("abstention, invalid and missing", () => {
    46	  it("are imputed with the dev prior, so abstaining cannot beat the prior", () => {
    47	    const outcomes: Direction3[] = ["none", "none", "down", "up", "none", "down"];
    48	    const prior = outcomes.map((o, i) => mk(i, o, PRIOR));
    49	    const abst = outcomes.map((o, i) => mk(i, o, null, "abstained"));
    50	    const inv = outcomes.map((o, i) => mk(i, o, null, "invalid"));
    51	    const sp = scoreProposer("prior", prior, PRIOR), sa = scoreProposer("abstain", abst, PRIOR), si = scoreProposer("inv", inv, PRIOR);
    52	    expect(sa.brierImputed).toBeCloseTo(sp.brierImputed!, 12);
    53	    expect(si.brierImputed).toBeCloseTo(sp.brierImputed!, 12);
    54	    expect(sa.coverage).toBe(0);
    55	    expect(sa.abstentionRate).toBe(1);
    56	    expect(si.invalid).toBe(6);
    57	    expect(sa.brierAnswered).toBeNull();
    58	    expect(sa.directionalAccuracy).toBeNull();
    59	  });
    60	  it("matched coverage restricts to the cases every comparator answered", () => {
    61	    const full = [mk(0, "up", { up: 1000, down: 0, none: 0 }), mk(1, "down", { up: 1000, down: 0, none: 0 }), mk(2, "none", { up: 0, down: 0, none: 1000 })];
    62	    const part = [mk(0, "up", { up: 1000, down: 0, none: 0 }), mk(1, "down", null, "abstained"), mk(2, "none", null, "abstained")];
    63	    const set = matchedCoverageSet({ full, part });
    64	    expect([...set]).toEqual(["c0"]);
    65	    expect(scoreProposer("full", full, PRIOR, set).directionalAccuracy).toBe(1);
    66	    expect(scoreProposer("full", full, PRIOR).directionalAccuracy).toBeCloseTo(2 / 3, 10);
    67	  });
    68	});
    69	
    70	describe("reliability, cost, stability, intervals", () => {
    71	  it("bins sum to the answered count", () => {
    72	    const cs = [mk(0, "up", { up: 900, down: 50, none: 50 }), mk(1, "none", { up: 340, down: 330, none: 330 }), mk(2, "up", null, "abstained"), mk(3, "down", { up: 100, down: 600, none: 300 })];
    73	    const r = reliabilityTable(cs);
    74	    expect(r.reduce((s, b) => s + b.n, 0)).toBe(3);
    75	    expect(r).toHaveLength(5);
    76	  });
    77	  it("costPerUseful is null when no case is non-null, else cell-steps over non-null cases", () => {
    78	    expect(costPerUseful(1000, ["none", "none"])).toBeNull();
    79	    expect(costPerUseful(1000, ["up", "none", "down", "none"])).toBe(500);
    80	  });
    81	  it("signAgreement counts matching labels", () => {
    82	    expect(signAgreement(["up", "none", "down"], ["up", "down", "down"])).toEqual({ n: 3, agree: 2, rate: 2 / 3 });
    83	    expect(signAgreement([], []).rate).toBeNull();
    84	  });
    85	  it("the bootstrap resamples families, and is skipped below the family minimum", () => {
    86	    const rows = (k: number) => Array.from({ length: k * 2 }, (_, i) => ({ familyId: `f${i % k}`, v: i % k }));
    87	    expect(familyBootstrap(familyMeans(rows(MIN_FAMILIES_FOR_INTERVALS - 1)), 200, 1)).toBeNull();
    88	    const iv = familyBootstrap(familyMeans(rows(12)), 500, 1)!;
    89	    expect(iv.mean).toBeCloseTo(5.5, 10);
    90	    expect(iv.lo).toBeLessThan(iv.mean);
    91	    expect(iv.hi).toBeGreaterThan(iv.mean);
    92	    expect(familyBootstrap(familyMeans(rows(12)), 500, 1)).toEqual(iv);
    93	  });
    94	});
     1	// The static notebook view, the outputs' byte reproducibility, and the comparison's shape.
     2	import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     3	import { sha256Text } from "../lib/propose.ts";
     4	import { CANONICAL_FILES, buildOutputs } from "../lib/prediction/output.ts";
     5	import { generate } from "../lib/prediction/run.ts";
     6	import { renderReport } from "../lib/prediction/report-html.ts";
     7	import { validatePredictionFile } from "../lib/prediction/validate.ts";
     8	import { tinyPipeline } from "./prediction-helpers.ts";
     9	
    10	const T = 240_000;
    11	
    12	describe("report.html", () => {
    13	  const { p, built } = tinyPipeline();
    14	  const html = built.files["report.html"];
    15	  const envs = built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    16	  it("contains every case id, no scripts and no external URLs", () => {
    17	    for (const e of envs) expect(html).toContain(`id="${e.caseId}"`);
    18	    expect(html).not.toMatch(/<script/i);
    19	    expect(html).not.toMatch(/https?:\/\//i);
    20	    expect(html).not.toMatch(/\son\w+=/i);
    21	  }, T);
    22	  it("shows failed predictions first and escapes the free-text reason", () => {
    23	    const rows = built.files["outcomes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    24	    const lines = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    25	    const evil = lines.map((l: { proposer: string; prediction: { reason: string } }) => (l.proposer === "fixedRule" ? { ...l, prediction: { ...l.prediction, reason: "<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>" } } : l));
    26	    const out = renderReport({ comparison: built.comparison, envelopes: envs, lines: evil as never, rows, minEffect: p.minEffect!, proposerIds: ["fixedRule", "numericTrend"] });
    27	    expect(out).not.toContain("<img src=x");
    28	    expect(out).toContain("&lt;img src=x");
    29	    const first = out.match(/<article[\s\S]*?<\/article>/)![0];
    30	    expect(first).toContain('<li class="bad">');
    31	    const lastArticle = [...out.matchAll(/<article[\s\S]*?<\/article>/g)].pop()![0];
    32	    const bads = (h: string) => (h.match(/<li class="bad">/g) ?? []).length;
    33	    expect(bads(first)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(bads(lastArticle));
    34	  }, T);
    35	  it("lists invalid predictions in their own section", () => {
    36	    const rows = built.files["outcomes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    37	    const lines = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    38	    const broken = lines.map((l: { proposer: string; prediction: Record<string, unknown> }, i: number) => (l.proposer === "fixedRule" && i % 5 === 0 ? { ...l, prediction: { ...l.prediction, probs: { up: 1, down: 1, none: 1 } } } : l));
    39	    const out = renderReport({ comparison: built.comparison, envelopes: envs, lines: broken as never, rows, minEffect: p.minEffect!, proposerIds: ["fixedRule"] });
    40	    expect(out).toMatch(/Invalid or missing predictions<\/h2><ul>/);
    41	  }, T);
    42	});
    43	
    44	describe("outputs", () => {
    45	  const { p, built } = tinyPipeline();
    46	  it("are byte-reproducible: a second generation gives identical canonical files", () => {
    47	    const again = buildOutputs(p, generate(p, p.families.map((f) => f.id), true));
    48	    for (const n of CANONICAL_FILES) expect(again.files[n], n).toBe(built.files[n]);
    49	    expect(again.canonicalSha256).toBe(built.canonicalSha256);
    50	  }, T);
    51	  it("canonical.sha256 lists exactly the canonical files with their hashes", () => {
    52	    const listed = built.canonicalSha256.trim().split("\n").map((l) => l.split("  "));
    53	    expect(listed.map((x) => x[1])).toEqual([...CANONICAL_FILES]);
    54	    for (const [h, n] of listed) expect(h).toBe(sha256Text(built.files[n]));
    55	  });
    56	  it("every proposer's file validates against the envelopes; shuffled never uses its own family; the oracle is absent from the test table", () => {
    57	    const envs = built.files["envelopes.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    58	    const lines = built.files["predictions.jsonl"].trim().split("\n").map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
    59	    const ids = new Set(lines.map((l: { proposer: string }) => l.proposer));
    60	    for (const id of ids) {
    61	      const r = validatePredictionFile(lines.filter((l: { proposer: string }) => l.proposer === id).map((l: { prediction: unknown }) => JSON.stringify(l.prediction)), envs);
    62	      expect(r.invalid + r.missing.length + r.extra.length, id).toBe(0);
    63	    }
    64	    expect(built.comparison.test.rows.map((r) => r.id)).not.toContain("mechanismRule");
    65	    expect(built.comparison.dev.rows.map((r) => r.id)).toContain("mechanismRule");
    66	    expect(built.comparison.test.rows.find((r) => r.id === "abstainAll")!.coverage).toBe(0);
    67	    expect(built.comparison.perFamily.test.intervals).toBe(false);
    68	  });
    69	  it("abstainAll scores exactly the prior (imputation) on the headline Brier", () => {
    70	    const prior = built.comparison.test.rows.find((r) => r.id === "priorMajority")!;
    71	    const ab = built.comparison.test.rows.find((r) => r.id === "abstainAll")!;
    72	    expect(ab.brierImputed).toBeCloseTo(prior.brierImputed!, 12);
    73	  });
    74	  it("the comparison states no model was run", () => {
    75	    expect(built.comparison.noModelStatement).toMatch(/No model call/);
    76	    expect(built.files["comparison.md"]).toContain("Trend-confound diagnostic");
    77	  });
    78	});
     1	// Spawned `deno run --no-lock` smoke test of the CLI: a tiny smoke run, then `validate` on its predictions, then the confirm refusals.
     2	import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
     3	import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
     4	import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
     5	import { join } from "node:path";
     6	import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
     7	import { afterAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     8	import { confirmRefusal, draftProtocol, protocolHash, splitPlanOf } from "../lib/prediction/run.ts";
     9	import { donorsFor } from "../lib/prediction/splits.ts";
    10	import { tinyProtocol } from "./prediction-helpers.ts";
    11	
    12	const root = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
    13	const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "predict-cli-"));
    14	afterAll(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));
    15	const deno = (args: string[]) => spawnSync("deno", ["run", "-A", "--no-lock", "tools/predict.ts", ...args], { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8" });
    16	
    17	describe("tools/predict.ts", () => {
    18	  const p = tinyProtocol();
    19	  p.families = p.families.slice(0, 4);
    20	  p.nDev = 2;
    21	  p.nQualify = 1;
    22	  p.donors = donorsFor(splitPlanOf(p), p.salt);
    23	  const protoPath = join(dir, "protocol.json");
    24	  writeFileSync(protoPath, JSON.stringify(p));
    25	
    26	  it("runs a tiny smoke stage, writes the canonical files, and validate accepts its predictions", () => {
    27	    const r = deno(["run", "--stage", "smoke", "--protocol", protoPath, "--out", join(dir, "out")]);
    28	    expect(r.status, r.stderr).toBe(0);
    29	    const meta = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, "out/meta.json"), "utf8"));
    30	    expect(meta.status).toBe("SMOKE");
    31	    expect(meta.statement).toMatch(/No model call/);
    32	    const v = deno(["validate", join(dir, "out/predictions.jsonl")]);
    33	    expect(v.status, v.stdout + v.stderr).toBe(0);
    34	    expect(v.stdout).toMatch(/fixedRule: \d+ lines, invalid 0, missing 0/);
    35	  }, 300_000);
    36	  it("validate fails on a corrupted line", () => {
    37	    const f = join(dir, "bad.jsonl");
    38	    const lines = readFileSync(join(dir, "out/predictions.jsonl"), "utf8").trim().split("\n");
    39	    writeFileSync(f, [lines[0].replace('"abstain":false', '"abstain":true'), ...lines.slice(1)].join("\n") + "\n");
    40	    const v = deno(["validate", f, "--envelopes", join(dir, "out/envelopes.jsonl")]);
    41	    expect(v.status).toBe(1);
    42	  }, 120_000);
    43	  it("refuses an unknown stage", () => {
    44	    const u = deno(["run", "--stage", "bogus", "--out", join(dir, "x")]);
    45	    expect(u.status).toBe(1);
    46	    expect(u.stderr).toMatch(/unknown --stage/);
    47	  }, 120_000);
    48	  it("the confirm gate refuses an unfrozen protocol, a missing freeze file and a hash mismatch", () => {
    49	    const d = draftProtocol();
    50	    expect(confirmRefusal(d, null)).toMatch(/not frozen/);
    51	    const f = { ...d, frozen: true, minEffect: 50, devPrior: { up: 100, down: 100, none: 800 } };
    52	    expect(confirmRefusal(f, null)).toMatch(/missing/);
    53	    expect(confirmRefusal(f, "0".repeat(64))).toMatch(/does not match/);
    54	    expect(confirmRefusal(f, protocolHash(f) + "\n")).toBeNull();
    55	    expect(confirmRefusal({ ...f, minEffect: 51 }, protocolHash(f))).toMatch(/does not match/);
    56	  });
    57	});

    31	export type Action = "feed" | "drain" | "lesion" | "control";
    32	export type Direction = "up" | "down";
    33	
    34	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35	// Canonical JSON and identity.
    36	
    37	/** Canonical JSON: keys sorted (UTF-16 order) at every depth, no whitespace, numbers as JSON.stringify writes them. Throws on a non-finite number, undefined, or a non-plain object. */
    38	export function canonical(v: unknown, path = "$"): string {
    39	  if (v === null || typeof v === "boolean" || typeof v === "string") return JSON.stringify(v);
    40	  if (typeof v === "number") {
    41	    if (!Number.isFinite(v)) throw new Error(`canonical: ${path} is not a finite number`);
    42	    return JSON.stringify(v);
    43	  }
    44	  if (Array.isArray(v)) return `[${v.map((x, i) => canonical(x, `${path}[${i}]`)).join(",")}]`;
    45	  if (typeof v === "object" && (Object.getPrototypeOf(v) === Object.prototype || Object.getPrototypeOf(v) === null)) {
    46	    const o = v as Record<string, unknown>;
    47	    return `{${Object.keys(o)
    48	      .sort()
    49	      .map((k) => {
    50	        if (o[k] === undefined) throw new Error(`canonical: ${path}.${k} is undefined`);
    51	        return `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonical(o[k], `${path}.${k}`)}`;
    52	      })
    53	      .join(",")}}`;
    54	  }
    55	  throw new Error(`canonical: ${path} has unsupported type ${typeof v}`);
    56	}
    57	const sha = (text: string): string => sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(text));
    58	/** SHA-256 hex of a text (additive export for tools/lib/prediction, which hashes its envelopes the same way). */
    59	export const sha256Text = sha;
    60	export const bodySha256 = (body: ProposalBody): string => sha(canonical(body));
    61	export const proposalId = (body: ProposalBody): string => bodySha256(body).slice(0, 16);
    62	/** The SHA-256 of a run spec's canonical JSON: two rings that propose the same run are one proposal (the note, "Which rings"). */
    63	export const specHash = (spec: RunSpec): string => sha(canonical(spec));
    64	
    65	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    66	// The notes file (tools/notebook.ts's .notes.json): only the fields the proposer uses, each checked.
   250	
   251	/** The spec keys a fork copies or derives; any other key in the source's spec is refused (the argv could not carry it). */
   252	const COPYABLE = new Set(["experiment", "presetId", "condition", "seed", "steps", "censusEvery", "deepEvery", "checkpointEvery", "activityThreshold", "lineageObs", "overrides"]);
   253	
   254	/**
   255	 * Why v0 cannot fork this source, or null (the note, "The vocabulary, in v0"): a pond world, a branch, a picked or pressured run,
   256	 * a metapopulation member, founder options, a run that already carries interventions, any other spec key tools/run.ts cannot
   257	 * pass, an override other than mutRate, an unfinished run, a continuation segment, and a rule, schema or metrics version other
   258	 * than this checkout's (the replay would not be the source's history).
   259	 */
   260	export function sourceRefusal(m: SourceManifest): string | null {
   261	  const s = m.spec as Record<string, unknown>;
   262	  if (m.cfg.pondPeriod !== undefined) return "the source is a pond world (v0 does not fork pond worlds: no feed, and picks are not in its vocabulary)";
   263	  if (s.branch !== undefined || m.branch !== undefined) return "the source is a branch run";
   264	  if (s.picked !== undefined || m.picks !== undefined) return "the source is a picked run";
   265	  if (s.pressures !== undefined) return "the source is a pressured run";
   266	  if (s.metapopulation !== undefined) return "the source belongs to a metapopulation";
   267	  if (s.soloFounder !== undefined || s.soloGenome !== undefined || s.founderSet !== undefined) return "the source was founded with founder options (soloFounder, soloGenome or founderSet)";
   268	  if (s.interventions !== undefined || m.interventions !== undefined) return "the source already carries interventions";
   269	  const extra = Object.keys(s).find((k) => !COPYABLE.has(k));
   270	  if (extra !== undefined) return `the source's spec has ${JSON.stringify(extra)}, which a v0 fork cannot carry`;
   271	  if (s.overrides !== undefined) {
   272	    const o = s.overrides as Record<string, unknown>;
   273	    if (!isObj(o) || Object.keys(o).length === 0 || Object.keys(o).some((k) => k !== "mutRate") || !isInt(o.mutRate)) return "the source's overrides are not a mutRate override alone";
   274	  }
   275	  if (s.activityThreshold !== undefined && !(isNum(s.activityThreshold) && (s.activityThreshold as number) > 0)) return "the source's activityThreshold is not a positive number";
   276	  if (m.summary === null || m.summary === undefined) return "the source run is not finished (its manifest has no summary)";
   277	  if (m.startStep !== undefined && m.startStep !== 0) return `the source starts at step ${JSON.stringify(m.startStep)}, not 0 (a continuation segment, not a whole history)`;
   278	  if (typeof m.initHash !== "string") return "the source's manifest has no initHash, so step 0 cannot be checked";
   279	  if (m.ruleVersion !== RULE_VERSION) return `the source ran under rule version ${JSON.stringify(m.ruleVersion)}, this checkout's is ${RULE_VERSION}`;
   280	  if (m.schemaVersion !== SCHEMA_VERSION) return `the source's schema version is ${JSON.stringify(m.schemaVersion)}, this checkout's is ${SCHEMA_VERSION}`;
   281	  if (((m.metricsVersion as number | undefined) ?? 1) !== METRICS_VERSION) return `the source's metrics version is ${JSON.stringify(m.metricsVersion ?? 1)}, this checkout's is ${METRICS_VERSION} (its series would not replay byte for byte)`;
   282	  return null;
   283	}
   284	
   285	/** The notes file describes this bundle (the note, "The prediction": run id, presetIdentity, steps, cadence, and the three files' byte sizes). Throws naming what differs. */
   286	export function bindNotesToManifest(notes: NotesFile, m: SourceManifest, sizes: Readonly<Record<(typeof BUNDLE_FILES)[number], number>>): void {
   287	  const diffs: string[] = [];
   288	  if (notes.run.id !== sourceRunId(m)) diffs.push(`run id (notes ${notes.run.id}, bundle ${sourceRunId(m)})`);
   289	  if (notes.run.presetIdentity !== m.presetIdentity) diffs.push(`presetIdentity (notes ${notes.run.presetIdentity}, bundle ${m.presetIdentity})`);
   290	  if (notes.run.steps !== m.spec.steps) diffs.push(`steps (notes ${notes.run.steps}, bundle ${m.spec.steps})`);
   291	  if (notes.run.censusEvery !== m.spec.censusEvery) diffs.push(`censusEvery (notes ${notes.run.censusEvery}, bundle ${m.spec.censusEvery})`);
   292	  for (const f of BUNDLE_FILES) {
   293	    const listed = notes.sources.find((s) => basename(s.path) === f)!.bytes;
   294	    if (listed !== sizes[f]) diffs.push(`${f} size (notes ${listed}, bundle ${sizes[f]})`);
   295	  }
   296	  if (diffs.length) throw new Error(`the notes file does not describe this bundle: ${diffs.join("; ")}`);
   297	}

   299	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   300	// Fork point, jar disc, rules.
   301	
   302	/**
   303	 * The fork step C for intervention step S: the largest verified checkpoint step strictly before S, else 0 (the note, "Where a fork
   304	 * starts"). `verified` is called on candidates from the largest down and says whether that checkpoint's file exists, decodes and
   305	 * hashes to its manifest entry; step 0 is checked separately (initHash).
   306	 */
   307	export function forkPoint(S: number, checkpoints: readonly { step: number }[], verified: (step: number) => boolean = () => true): number {
   308	  const steps = [...new Set(checkpoints.map((c) => c.step))].filter((t) => t > 0 && t < S).sort((x, y) => y - x);
   309	  for (const t of steps) if (verified(t)) return t;
   310	  return 0;
   311	}
   312	
   313	/** The jar disc (the note, "Where an intervention lands"): one centre per tile, row-major, radius maxLesionRadius. */
   314	export function jarDisc(cfg: WorldConfig): { x: number; y: number; r: number }[] {
   315	  const out: { x: number; y: number; r: number }[] = [];
   316	  const r = maxLesionRadius(cfg);
   317	  for (let ty = 0; ty < cfg.tilesY; ty++) for (let tx = 0; tx < cfg.tilesX; tx++) out.push({ x: tx * cfg.tileW + Math.floor(cfg.tileW / 2), y: ty * cfg.tileH + Math.floor(cfg.tileH / 2), r });
   318	  return out;
   319	}
   320	
   321	/** Cells in a disc of radius r (dx² + dy² ≤ r², as packages/schema/src/feed.ts and a lesion count them; r ≤ maxLesionRadius, so no cell twice). */
   322	export function discCells(r: number): number {
   323	  let n = 0;
   324	  for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= r * r) n++;
   325	  return n;
   326	}
   327	
   328	/** The jar disc's share of the world's cells, in whole percent (77 on a 256 × 256 tile). */
   329	export function jarShare(cfg: WorldConfig): number {
   330	  return Math.round((100 * cfg.tilesX * cfg.tilesY * discCells(maxLesionRadius(cfg))) / cellCount(cfg));
   331	}
   332	
   333	/** A, the feed and drain amount: the preset's init.nutrient, or null when it is not in 1..FEED_MAX (no feed or drain then). */
   334	export function feedAmount(presetId: string): number | null {
   335	  const a = PRESETS.find((p) => p.id === presetId)?.init.nutrient;
   336	  return isInt(a) && a >= 1 && a <= FEED_MAX ? a : null;
   337	}

   380	}
   381	
   382	/** Deterministic Fisher-Yates over `[0, n)`, keyed on `seed` (this file's own
   383	 * lowbias32/draw, no Math.random -- same PRNG convention as everywhere else
   384	 * in this file). */
   385	function shuffledIndices(n: number, seed: number): number[] {
   386	  const idx = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => i);
   387	  const base = lowbias32(seed ^ 0x9e3779b1);
   388	  for (let i = n - 1; i > 0; i--) {
   389	    const j = draw(base, i) % (i + 1);
   390	    [idx[i], idx[j]] = [idx[j], idx[i]];
   391	  }
   392	  return idx;
   393	}
   394	
   395	export function cloneState(s: WorldState): WorldState {
   396	  return { ...s, cells: s.cells.slice(), genome: s.genome.slice(), flux: s.flux.slice() };
   397	}
   398	
   399	export function isLiving(s: WorldState, i: number): boolean {
   400	  const n = cellCount(s.cfg);
   401	  return s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] !== 0 || s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i] !== 0;
   402	}
     1	import { cellCount, type WorldConfig } from "./config.ts";
     2	import { CH, G, GENOME_CHANNELS } from "./layout.ts";
     3	import type { WorldState } from "./world.ts";
     4	
     5	export interface Totals {
     6	  A: bigint;
     7	  B: bigint;
     8	  C: bigint;
     9	  P: bigint;
    10	  E: bigint;
    11	  S: bigint;
    12	  matter: bigint;
    13	  /** Chemical potential + free pools + signal. */
    14	  energy: bigint;
    15	  living: number;
    16	}
    17	
    18	export function sumChannel(cells: Uint32Array, n: number, ch: number): bigint {
    19	  let acc = 0;
    20	  let big = 0n;
    21	  const end = (ch + 1) * n;
    22	  for (let i = ch * n; i < end; i++) {
    23	    acc += cells[i];
    24	    if (acc > 2 ** 50) {
    25	      big += BigInt(acc);
    26	      acc = 0;
    27	    }
    28	  }
    29	  return big + BigInt(acc);
    30	}
    31	
    32	export function totalsOf(cfg: WorldConfig, cells: Uint32Array): Omit<Totals, "living"> {
    33	  const n = cellCount(cfg);
    34	  const A = sumChannel(cells, n, CH.A);
    35	  const B = sumChannel(cells, n, CH.B);
    36	  const C = sumChannel(cells, n, CH.C);
    37	  const P = sumChannel(cells, n, CH.P);
    38	  const E = sumChannel(cells, n, CH.E);
    39	  const S = sumChannel(cells, n, CH.S);
    40	  const energy = BigInt(cfg.eA) * A + BigInt(cfg.eB) * B + BigInt(cfg.eC) * C + BigInt(cfg.eP) * P + E + S;
    41	  return { A, B, C, P, E, S, matter: A + B + C + P, energy };
    42	}
    43	
    44	/**
    45	 * Energy ledger residual: (content now + heat exported) - (content at start + light absorbed).
    46	 * Exactly zero when the rules conserve energy.
    47	 */
    48	export function ledgerResidual(start: { energy: bigint }, s: WorldState): bigint {
    49	  const now = totalsOf(s.cfg, s.cells);
    50	  return now.energy + s.heatOut - (start.energy + s.lightIn);
    51	}
    52	
    53	/** Order-dependent 64-bit digest of a word array, as 16 hex chars. */
    54	export function digestWords(words: Uint32Array, h1 = 0x811c9dc5, h2 = 0x01000193): [number, number] {
    55	  for (let i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
    56	    const w = words[i];
    57	    h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ w, 0x85ebca6b) >>> 0;
    58	    h1 = ((h1 << 13) | (h1 >>> 19)) >>> 0;
    59	    h2 = Math.imul(h2 + w + i, 0xc2b2ae35) >>> 0;
    60	    h2 = (h2 ^ (h2 >>> 16)) >>> 0;
    61	  }
    62	  return [h1, h2];
    63	}
    64	
    65	/**
    66	 * Genome words of empty cells (lineage 0:0) are never read by the rules and
    67	 * may hold stale data; the canonical form zeroes them.
    68	 */
    69	export function canonicalGenome(genome: Uint32Array): Uint32Array {
    70	  const n = genome.length / GENOME_CHANNELS;
    71	  let dirty = false;
    72	  for (let i = 0; i < n && !dirty; i++)
    73	    if ((genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] | genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i]) === 0)
    74	      for (let g = 2; g < GENOME_CHANNELS; g++) if (genome[g * n + i] !== 0) { dirty = true; break; }
    75	  if (!dirty) return genome;
    76	  const out = genome.slice();
    77	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
    78	    if ((out[G.LIN_HI * n + i] | out[G.LIN_LO * n + i]) === 0) for (let g = 2; g < GENOME_CHANNELS; g++) out[g * n + i] = 0;
    79	  return out;
    80	}
    81	
    82	/** Config serialised with sorted keys (stable across key order). */
    83	export function canonicalConfig(c: WorldState["cfg"]): string {
    84	  const o = c as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
    85	  return JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(o).sort().map((k) => [k, o[k]])));
    86	}
    87	
    88	const u64Words = (v: bigint) => [Number(v & 0xffffffffn), Number((v >> 32n) & 0xffffffffn)];
    89	
    90	function stateHashWords(s: WorldState): [number, number] {
    91	  const cfg = new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalConfig(s.cfg));
    92	  const cfgWords = new Uint32Array(Math.ceil(cfg.length / 4));
    93	  new Uint8Array(cfgWords.buffer).set(cfg);
    94	  let [a, b] = digestWords(Uint32Array.of(cfg.length, ...cfgWords));
    95	  [a, b] = digestWords(Uint32Array.of(s.step, ...u64Words(s.lightIn), ...u64Words(s.heatOut), ...s.flux.flatMap(u64Words)), a, b);
    96	  [a, b] = digestWords(s.cells, a, b);
    97	  [a, b] = digestWords(canonicalGenome(s.genome), a, b);
    98	  return [a, b];
    99	}
   100	
   101	const hex = (a: number, b: number) => a.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") + b.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
   102	
   103	/**
   104	 * Canonical 64-bit digest of every persistent field: config, step, cells,
   105	 * canonical genome and the full ledger (light, heat, fluxes). Physics-only —
   106	 * used for pinned hashes, segment `startHash`/`startFrom` continuity checks
   107	 * and same-device replay verification. Does not cover observer state; see
   108	 * `artifactDigest` for the digest that does.
   109	 */
   110	export function stateHash(s: WorldState): string {
   111	  const [a, b] = stateHashWords(s);
   112	  return hex(a, b);
   113	}
   114	
   115	/**
   116	 * Deterministic JSON for digesting: object keys sorted recursively (arrays
   117	 * keep their order, since it's meaningful there). The observer payload is
   118	 * caller-constructed, not arbitrary user JSON, so this only needs to be
   119	 * stable across the field-insertion-order variance between callers, not
   120	 * hostile input.
   121	 */
   122	function canonicalize(v: unknown): unknown {
   123	  if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(canonicalize);
   124	  if (v && typeof v === "object") {
   125	    const o = v as Record<string, unknown>;
    38	 * ledger failure (packages/runner/src/interventions.ts's `applyScheduled` wraps only this as an `InterventionError`).
    39	 */
    40	export class FeedRefusedError extends Error {
    41	  constructor(message: string) {
    42	    super(message);
    43	    this.name = "FeedRefusedError";
    44	  }
    45	}
    46	
    47	/** Why `amount` cannot be fed over `radius` to a world of this config, or null. */
    48	export function feedError(cfg: WorldConfig, amount: number, radius: number): string | null {
    49	  // A non-finite radius would clamp to NaN, feed nothing, and be logged as null: a log that replays differently.
    50	  if (!Number.isFinite(radius)) return `feed radius must be a finite number, got ${radius}`;
    51	  if (!Number.isInteger(amount) || amount === 0 || Math.abs(amount) > FEED_MAX) return `feed amount must be a non-zero integer within ±${FEED_MAX} per cell, got ${amount}`;
    52	  // Each pond's matter is fixed by its cycle and checked against the history's start (PondContext).
    53	  if (cfg.pondPeriod !== undefined) return "feeding is not defined for pond worlds: the pond cycle fixes each pond's matter";
    54	  return null;
    55	}
    56	
    57	/**
    58	 * Feeds the nutrient channel `A` (one word per cell) in place. `matterNow` is the world's total
    59	 * matter before the call; a feed that would take it past MATTER_MAX is refused and changes nothing.
    60	 */
    61	export function feedNutrient(cfg: WorldConfig, A: Uint32Array, matterNow: bigint, cx: number, cy: number, radius: number, amount: number): FeedResult {
    62	  const err = feedError(cfg, amount, radius);
    63	  if (err) throw new FeedRefusedError(err);
    64	  const W = worldW(cfg), n = cellCount(cfg), H = n / W;
    65	  if (A.length !== n) throw new Error(`feed: nutrient channel has ${A.length} cells, expected ${n}`);
    66	  if (!Number.isInteger(cx) || !Number.isInteger(cy) || cx < 0 || cx >= W || cy < 0 || cy >= H) throw new Error(`feed centre (${cx}, ${cy}) is outside the world`);
    67	  const r = clampLesionRadius(cfg, radius);
    68	  const { tileW, tileH } = cfg;
    69	  const tx = Math.floor(cx / tileW), ty = Math.floor(cy / tileH);
    70	  const disc: number[] = [];
    71	  for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++)
    72	    for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
    73	      if (dx * dx + dy * dy > r * r) continue;
    74	      const lx = (cx - tx * tileW + dx + tileW) % tileW, ly = (cy - ty * tileH + dy + tileH) % tileH;
    75	      disc.push((ty * tileH + ly) * W + tx * tileW + lx);
    76	    }
    77	  let matter = 0;
    78	  if (amount > 0) {
    79	    matter = amount * disc.length;
    80	    if (matterNow + BigInt(matter) > BigInt(MATTER_MAX)) throw new FeedRefusedError(`feed refused: ${matter} quanta would take the world past MATTER_MAX (${MATTER_MAX}); it holds ${matterNow}`);
    81	    for (const j of disc) A[j] += amount;
    82	  } else {
    83	    for (const j of disc) {
    84	      const take = Math.min(A[j], -amount);
    85	      A[j] -= take;
    86	      matter -= take;
    87	    }
    88	  }
    89	  return { radius: r, cells: disc.length, matter, energy: BigInt(matter) * BigInt(cfg.eA) };
    90	}
    91	
    92	/** `feedNutrient` on a whole state, in place: the reference a host's own path (GpuSim.feed) must equal. */
    93	export function applyFeed(state: WorldState, cx: number, cy: number, radius: number, amount: number): FeedResult {
    94	  const n = cellCount(state.cfg);
    95	  return feedNutrient(state.cfg, state.cells.subarray(CH.A * n, (CH.A + 1) * n), totalsOf(state.cfg, state.cells).matter, cx, cy, radius, amount);
    96	}
    97	
    98	/** Net quanta of nutrient a log's feeds added (negative: drained). */
    99	export function fedMatter(interventions: readonly { kind: string; matter?: number }[]): number {
   100	  let total = 0;
   101	  for (const i of interventions) if (i.kind === "feed") total += i.matter ?? 0;
   102	  return total;
   103	}
   887	 * waste; their excess chemical energy and the free pool leave as heat.
   888	 * Applied between steps, identically on CPU and GPU.
   889	 */
   890	export function applyLesion(sim: RefSim, cx: number, cy: number, radius: number): void {
   891	  const r = clampLesionRadius(sim.cfg, radius);
   892	  const { cells, genome } = sim.state;
   893	  const c = sim.cfg;
   894	  const n = sim.n;
   895	  let heat = 0n;
   896	  for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
   897	    for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
   898	      if (dx * dx + dy * dy > r * r) continue;
   899	      const j = sim.nb(cx, cy, dx, dy);
   900	      const B = cells[CH.B * n + j], P = cells[CH.P * n + j], E = cells[CH.E * n + j];
   901	      heat += BigInt(B) * BigInt(c.eB - c.eC) + BigInt(P) * BigInt(c.eP - c.eC) + BigInt(E);
   902	      cells[CH.C * n + j] = addu(cells[CH.C * n + j], addu(B, P));
   903	      cells[CH.B * n + j] = 0;
   904	      cells[CH.P * n + j] = 0;
   905	      cells[CH.E * n + j] = 0;
   906	      cells[CH.MOT * n + j] = MOT_ZERO;
   907	      genome[G.LIN_HI * n + j] = 0;
   908	      genome[G.LIN_LO * n + j] = 0;
   909	    }
   910	  }
   911	  sim.state.heatOut += heat;
   912	}
   375	}
   376	const drain = { action: "drain", direction: "down" } as const;
   377	const feed = { action: "feed", direction: "up" } as const;
   378	const lesion = { action: "lesion", direction: "down" } as const;
   379	const control = { action: "control" } as const;
   380	/** The note's table, one row per (signal, side), in SIGNALS order. */
   381	export const RULES: Readonly<Record<string, Readonly<Record<Side, RuleRow>>>> = Object.freeze({
   382	  livingCells: { high: drain, low: feed },
   383	  individuals: { high: drain, low: feed },
   384	  meanMass: { high: drain, low: feed },
   385	  lineages: { high: lesion, low: control },
   386	  lineageShannon: { high: lesion, low: control },
   387	  patternEntropy: { high: control, low: control },
   388	  bioticRecycling: { high: control, low: control },
   389	  maxGeneration: { high: lesion, low: control },
   390	  dominantShare: { high: lesion, low: control },
   391	  fissions: { high: drain, low: feed },
   392	  fusions: { high: drain, low: feed },
   393	  buddings: { high: drain, low: feed },
   394	  mutations: { high: drain, low: feed },
   395	});
   396	export function ruleFor(signal: string, side: Side): RuleRow {
   397	  const row = RULES[signal]?.[side];
   398	  if (!row) throw new Error(`no rule for ${signal} ${side}`);
   399	  return row;
   400	}

     1	// Shared page shell for tools/report-html.ts's four track renderers
     2	// (tools/lib/report-html/{nullcal,individuality,anticipation,biogeography}.ts).
     3	// Everything here is a pure string-building function -- no Date/Math.random,
     4	// no DOM -- so a renderer built entirely from these helpers is a deterministic
     5	// function of its input data (tools/test/report-html/determinism.test.ts).
     6	//
     7	// Output is an HTML FRAGMENT meant to be published as a claude.ai Artifact:
     8	// no <!doctype>/<html>/<head>/<body>, starting with <title> then <style>.
     9	// This file owns
    10	// the CSS custom-property tokens, the page shell, the header block (title,
    11	// question, DATA STATUS chip, source path + SHA-256, one static per-status
    12	// sentence, and a mechanical "Key numbers" definition list), and the
    13	// figure/numbers-table helpers every track renderer composes with.
    14	//
    15	// Architectural rule: a
    16	// renderer never writes a free-form sentence that interprets or characterizes
    17	// the data -- no "the gate holds", "not enough time", "this shows autonomy".
    18	// Every number a renderer wants to surface up top goes in the header's Key
    19	// numbers <dl> (`keyNumbersList` below) as a {label, value} pair: the label is
    20	// static text naming the exact statistic/scope, the value is read verbatim
    21	// off the input (or "unavailable" when null/missing -- never a guessed
    22	// cause). The only prose sentence tied to DATA STATUS is the single static
    23	// one per status defined in `STATUS_SENTENCE` below, identical across every
    24	// track. Captions/caveats still exist but are held to the same rule: a
    25	// caption describes only what is plotted and how to read it, and a caveat
    26	// states only a method fact true for any valid input (see each track's own
    27	// header doc for its caveats' pointer to the relevant docs/*.md).
    28	
    29	/** Escapes `&<>"'` for safe use in HTML text and attribute contexts. This is
    30	 * the ONLY string-escaping helper in this module family -- every renderer
    31	 * must route untrusted/data-derived text (labels, ids, paths, numbers
    32	 * formatted as strings) through this before interpolating into markup. */
    33	export function escapeHtml(s: string): string {
    34	  return s
    35	    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    36	    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    37	    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    38	    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    39	    .replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
    40	}
    41	
    42	export type DataStatus = "REAL" | "PILOT" | "SMOKE";
    43	
    44	export interface SourceRef {
    45	  /** The input file path as given on the CLI (relative or absolute), shown
    46	   * verbatim in the header -- never resolved to an absolute path, so the
    47	   * page stays deterministic across machines/checkouts. */
    48	  path: string;
    49	  /** Lowercase hex SHA-256 of the file's raw bytes. */
    50	  sha256: string;
